In the two-emitter limit, accumulated squeezing drives excitation buildup and generates a nonzero connected second-order correlation. For many-emitter arrays, tuning the interparticle distance switches the dominant local second-order correlation between the bulk and boundary regions, and enables an analogous spatial control of genuine third-order connected correlations. In the steady state, the averaged second-order correlation exhibits sign-changing and nonmonotonic behavior in the squeezing--interparticle distance parameter space, whereas the third-order connected correlation is strongly enhanced by increasing the squeezing parameter. These results identify nonlinear waveguide QED as a tunable platform for spatially controlling connected quantum correlations and provide insights into correlation engineering in open quantum optical arrays. We further show that this local contrast remains visible in both odd and even number of atomic arrays.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum key distribution over a 2 km free-space channel with a high secure key rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24118)
*Kyungdeuk Park,Dongkyu Kim,Dong-Gil Im,Yonggi Jo,Jisu Kim,Jonguk Choi,Yong Sup Ihn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验展示了基于诱骗态BB84协议的100 MHz重复频率、2.5 ns脉宽的2 km户外自由空间QKD系统，采用快速转向镜和位置敏感探测器实现主动光束漂移校正，在约3.3%的量子误码率下获得164.8 kbps的安全密钥率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间QKD具有移动性和部署灵活性等优势，但面临多光子漏洞、空间模式失配和大气湍流引起的光束波动等实际挑战，需要解决这些问题以实现实用化部署。

Method: 采用诱骗态BB84协议，系统工作于100 MHz重复频率、2.5 ns脉冲宽度，通过基于快速转向镜和位置敏感探测器的主动光束漂移校正方案来抑制大气湍流影响，在2 km室外信道上进行实验验证。

Result: 在2 km自由空间信道上实现了164.8 kbps的安全密钥率，量子误码率约为3.3%。

Conclusion: 该演示为在真实湍流环境中部署高速率、远距离自由空间量子通信提供了实用的技术框架。

Abstract: Free-space quantum key distribution (QKD) provides crucial advantages, including mobility and deployment flexibility, for securing next-generation communication networks. However, practical free-space implementations face major challenges, such as muilti-photon vulnerabilities, spatial mode mismatch, and atmospheric turbulence-induced beam fluctuations. In this work, we experimentally demonstrate a free-space decoy-state BB84 QKD system operating at a 100 MHz repetition rate with a 2.5 ns pulse width over a 2 km outdoor channel. By employing an active beam-wander correction based on fast-steering mirrors (FSMs) and position sensitive detectors (PSDs) con?figuration, our system achieves a secure key rate of 164.8 kbps under a quantum bit error rate (QBER) of approximately 3.3 %. This demonstration provides a practical framework for deploying high-rate, long-distance free-space quantum communication in realistic turbulence environments.

</details>


### [105] [Tree Tensor Network Reservoir Computing: Hierarchical Ensemble with Invariant Phase Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24127)
*Daiki Sasaki,Chih-Chieh Chen,Tomah Sogabe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出树张量网络储层计算（TTN-RC）框架，利用TTN层级结构作为随机储层，通过分层集成方法控制输出指数发散/收敛，在NARMA基准测试中性能优于传统回声状态网络，并理论推导了渐近稳定边界σ_T=√2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储层计算（如回声状态网络）在处理高阶非线性和长程依赖任务时性能受限，而量子启发的张量网络方法潜力未被充分挖掘，但存在输出指数集中或发散的问题亟待解决。

Method: 采用树张量网络（TTN）的层级结构作为随机储层；引入分层集成方法，将固定大小储层划分为多个独立子储层以控制输出信号强度；基于储层雅可比矩阵推导期望收缩率，并建立储层状态统计的均场描述。

Result: 在NARMA基准测试中，TTN-RC达到与ESN竞争或更优的性能，尤其在需要高阶非线性处理和长上下文依赖的任务上表现突出；理论分析表明在大树尺寸极限下渐近稳定边界为σ_T=√2，多个理论指标在此处收敛。

Conclusion: TTN-RC提供了基于张量网络的储层计算设计原则，阐明了层级储层拓扑结构如何控制稳定性与非线性信息处理能力，为量子启发式时序预测方法奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We propose Tree Tensor Network Reservoir Computing (TTN-RC), a quantum-inspired reservoir computing framework for time-series prediction that uses the hierarchical structure of Tree Tensor Networks as a random reservoir. To control the exponential concentration or divergence of TTN outputs, we introduce a hierarchical ensemble method that partitions a fixed-size reservoir into multiple independent sub-reservoirs. In the tested NARMA benchmarks, TTN-RC achieves competitive or improved performance compared with conventional Echo State Networks, especially for tasks requiring higher-order nonlinear processing and longer contextual dependence. We also derive an expected contraction rate based on the reservoir Jacobian and develop a mean-field description of the reservoir-state statistics. These analyses identify an asymptotic stability boundary at $σ_{T}=\sqrt{2}$ in the large per-tree-size limit, where several theoretical indicators converge. Our results provide a design principle for tensor-network-based reservoir computing and clarify how hierarchical reservoir topology controls stability and nonlinear information processing.

</details>


### [106] [Local Universality and Structural Certificates for Minimal Fixed-Depth Two-Qutrit Gate Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24129)
*Yurui Liu,Ruoting Dou,Peng Xu,Xinsheng Tan,Shengjun Wu,Yang Yu,Zeng-Bing Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种维度饱和的固定核心拟设，通过四个固定的双qutrit核心与五个可调局部层交织，在参数计数最小的深度上实现了局部普适性。论文给出了可验证的局部普适性证书，构造了精确的Clifford字核心，分类了2304种辛作用，并证明了复对称核心的结构障碍。同时，通过硬件动机的超导核心数值验证了高保真度（F_avg≥0.999）和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在参数计数最小的维度饱和深度上，固定核心架构能否实现局部普适性，填补理论分析与实际硬件实现之间的空白。

Method: 1. 提出四个固定双qutrit核心与五个可调局部层交织的架构；2. 利用右平凡化微分构造光滑映射Φ_K，给出局部普适性的可验证证书；3. 构造Clifford字核心，利用Pauli标签分裂使恒等点微分成为精确等距；4. 分类满足分裂准则的2304种辛作用；5. 证明复对称核心恒等点微分秩至多为78的结构障碍；6. 通过硬件动机的超导核心进行数值验证，包括Haar随机目标测试、采样Jacobian秩、结构化目标和鲁棒性诊断。

Result: 1. 构造了精确的Clifford字核心及其2304种辛作用分类；2. 证明复对称核心存在恒等点微分秩≤78的结构障碍；3. 超导核心硬件验证：非对称、非交换驱动，F_avg≥0.999（1000个Haar随机目标），Jacobian秩、结构化目标和鲁棒性诊断均表现良好。

Conclusion: 在参数计数最小的维度饱和深度上建立了局部普适性，提供了精确的Clifford证书和硬件动机的数值案例研究，并将精确局部证书与更广泛的合成性能数值证据区分开来。

Abstract: We study a dimension-saturating fixed-core ansatz in which four copies of a fixed, non-tunable two-qutrit core $K\in SU(9)$ are interleaved with five adjustable local layers from $L=SU(3)\otimes SU(3)$. Since $\dim SU(9)=80$ and $5\dim L=80$, this is the shortest fixed-core architecture not excluded by parameter counting. We formulate the smooth map $Φ_K:L^5\to SU(9)$ and use its right-trivialized differential to give verifiable certificates for local universality. We construct an explicit Clifford-word core whose Pauli-label splitting makes the identity-point differential an exact isometry, and we classify all 2304 symplectic actions satisfying the same splitting criterion. We also prove a structural obstruction for an important symmetry class: every complex-symmetric core $K=K^{T}$, including every core generated by a time-independent real-symmetric Hamiltonian in the chosen computational basis, has identity-point differential rank at most 78; hence any full-rank certificate for such a core must occur away from that point. We then assess a hardware-motivated superconducting core generated by a noncommuting, temporally asymmetric drive. Direct calculation verifies $K_{\rm sc}\neq K_{\rm sc}^{\mathsf T}$, and the core achieves $F_{\rm avg}\ge 0.999$ for all 1000 Haar-random targets tested under the stated restart protocol. We also report favorable sampled Jacobian-rank, structured-target, and robustness diagnostics. These results establish local universality at the parameter-counting-minimal, dimension-saturating depth, with an exact Clifford certificate complemented by a hardware-motivated numerical case study. Throughout, we separate exact local certificates from numerical evidence for broader synthesis performance.

</details>


### [107] [Parallelizable Exact Synthesis of Quantum Circuits via Semi-Tensor Product](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24195)
*Chenjian Li,Dingchao Gao,Xiangzhen Zhou,Ji Guan,Pengcheng Zhu,Zhufei Chu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于半张量积（STP）理论的CNOT和相位多项式电路并行精确综合框架，通过枚举无向部分门拓扑并分别求解缺失的门方向，实现了两阶段并行化。在32个worker下达到12.8倍并行加速，STP方法在小规模实例上比基于SAT的基线快100-1000倍，在QASMBench电路优化工作流中中位数加速1.91倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精确综合方法存在编码开销大、并行可扩展性差和内存瓶颈等问题，亟需一种高效的并行精确综合方案。

Method: 基于半张量积（STP）矩阵理论，通过枚举无向部分门拓扑并分别求解缺失门方向，实现两阶段并行化；将电路语义转换为规范STP公式，通过从右到左的因式分解过程判定可行性并去除不可行的方向分配。

Result: 32个worker下实现12.8倍并行加速；STP方法在随机小规模实例上比SAT基线快100-1000倍；在QASMBench真实电路优化工作流中，89%的案例优于SAT方法，中位数加速1.91倍。

Conclusion: STP-based并行精确综合框架在效率和可扩展性上显著优于传统SAT方法，在量子编译和电路重综合优化中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Exact synthesis is a useful tool in quantum compilation by providing optimal alternative implementations of small circuit shards and is widely used as a circuit re-synthesis optimization kernel. However, existing exact synthesis methods suffer from encoding overhead, poor parallel scalability, and memory bottlenecks. This paper introduces a parallel exact synthesis framework for CNOT and phase polynomial circuits, which is based on the semi-tensor product (STP) theory of matrices. By enumerating undirected partial-gate topologies and solving the missing gate directions separately, we are able to parallelize both stages and achieve a parallel speedup of up to $12.8\times$ with 32 workers on this NP-hard problem. More specifically, for each topology, the circuit semantics are converted into canonical STP formulas, and feasibility is decided by a right-to-left factorization procedure that removes infeasible direction assignments. On randomly generated synthesis targets, STP is typically $100\times-1000\times$ faster than the SAT-based baseline on small instances, and remains competitive for more difficult instances. When integrated in a real-world circuit optimization workflow, our algorithm outperforms the SAT-based approach on 89% of cases in QASMBench, and achieves a median speedup of $1.91\times$.

</details>


### [108] [MPStab: an hybrid stabilizers tensor-network quantum circuit simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24258)
*Giulio Crognaletti,Mattia Robbiano,Michele Grossi,Matteo Robbiati*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MPStab，一个结合稳定子与张量网络的混合量子电路模拟器，用于在经典计算机上高效模拟量子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发经典计算机模拟量子系统的技术有两个关键原因：1）经典计算机在某些配置下非常有效且将持续有效；2）探索经典计算的极限有助于识别量子计算机的优势区间。

Method: MPStab基于稳定子与张量网络的混合形式体系，结合了两种方法的优势来实现量子电路的模拟。

Result: 展示了MPStab软件包的核心功能，并在多个有趣的模拟场景下评估了其性能表现。

Conclusion: MPStab作为一种混合模拟器，能够利用经典计算的有效性，同时为探索量子计算的优势区间提供了工具。

Abstract: The development of techniques for simulating quantum systems using classical computers is a paramount task for two primary reasons: i) there exist configurations for which classical computers are remarkably effective and will continue to be so, and ii) exploring the limits of classical computation facilitates the identification of the regimes of competence for quantum computers. In this work, we present MPStab, a quantum circuit simulator based on a hybrid formalism combining stabilizers and tensor networks, recently introduced in Ref. [1]. We present the package, its core functionalities, and explore its performances in a few interesting simulation regimes.

</details>


### [109] [Systematic Experiment Tracking in Quantum Software: A Case Study of Reservoir Computing with Error Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24264)
*Otso Kinanen,Valter Uotila,Vlad Stirbu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了量子软件开发中的实验追踪问题，指出量子计算的独特需求（硬件实验性、软件不断演进）要求对量子执行过程进行监控、聚合边际收益并检测错误源。通过量子储备池计算用于混沌时间序列预测的案例研究，详细展示了量子软件开发过程中的实验追踪方法，并将其推广到更广泛的量子开发流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算日益普及，开发者来自广泛领域，但现有实践和工具未能充分解决量子软件开发中实验追踪的特殊需求，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先阐述实验追踪的概念及量子计算对其提出的特殊要求，接着通过量子储备池计算用于混沌时间序列预测（含错误缓解）的案例研究，详细描述量子软件开发流程中的实验追踪方式，最后将经验推广至通用量子开发过程。

Result: 提出了量子软件开发中实验追踪的具体实践方法，包括监控量子执行、聚合边际收益、检测错误源，并通过案例验证了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 量子软件开发需要针对其硬件实验性和软件演进特性建立专门的实验追踪机制，本文提出的方法可推广至更广泛的量子开发流程中。

Abstract: Quantum computers are more widely available than ever, making the field more accessible and widespread. Practitioners are coming from a wide range of domains, conducting experiments and research using quantum computing approaches across a variety of problems. The current literature suggests that developers follow certain methodologies in quantum software development, often with a matching set of tools provided. Yet with the novel paradigm, there are areas that remain unaddressed in practices and tools. In this article, we go into the details of experiment tracking in quantum software development. We explain the basic concept of experiment tracking and detail how, in essence, quantum computing sets demands on tracking practices. Given the experimental state of hardware and the constantly evolving software, quantum execution must be monitored, marginal gains aggregated for the best outcome, and error sources detected. In our case study, quantum reservoir computing for chaotic time series data prediction with error mitigation, we present a detailed quantum software development process and describe how experiments can be tracked throughout development. We then generalize this knowledge into the broader quantum development process.

</details>


### [110] [Coherence as a resource for $N$-box and quantum pigeonhole paradoxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24282)
*Som Kanjilal,Rafael Wagner,Ernesto F. Galvão*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究前选择和後選擇（PPS）悖論，特別是對非邏輯型PPS悖論（如N-box和量子鴿巢悖論）進行了分析。研究發現，這些悖論成立當且僅當存在針對單一算符的弱值異常，而弱值異常需要前選擇和後選擇狀態在中间測量基下具有相干性。這澄清了這些場景中不同非經典性概念的作用，並突顯了與（嚴格）經典解釋所需的定量偏離。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 釐清非邏輯型前選擇和後選擇（PPS）悖論（如N-box和量子鴿巢悖論）與量子非經典性（如相干性和弱值異常）之間的確切關係，並區分邏輯型與非邏輯型PPS悖論在非經典性根源上的差異。

Method: 通過證明每個悖論成立當且僅當存在針對特定單一算符的弱值異常，並利用弱值異常需要前選擇和後選擇狀態在中间測量基下具有相干性這一事實，來建立悖論與相干性之間的充要條件。

Result: 證明了N-box和量子鴿巢悖論都需要前選擇和後選擇狀態在中间測量基下具有相干性；每個悖論等價於一個特定算符的弱值異常；澄清了這些場景中非經典性源於弱值異常所需的一致性破壞，而非邏輯矛盾。

Conclusion: 非邏輯型PPS悖論的非經典性根源在於弱值異常所需的相干性，而非邏輯矛盾；這與邏輯型PPS悖論（連結到上下文性）有本質區別，並突顯了量子非經典性可以通過非相干子理論的定量偏離來理解。

Abstract: Pre- and post-selection (PPS) paradoxes are striking demonstrations of quantum nonclassicality. Logical PPS paradoxes, where inferences made with the Aharonov-Bergmann-Lebowitz (ABL) rule are exactly 0 or 1, are linked to contextuality. Non-logical paradoxes lack this strong signature. In this work, we analyse more general, non-logical PPS scenarios involving mixed pre- and post-selected states. We show that two such scenarios, the $N$-box and quantum pigeonhole paradoxes, require coherence of both pre- and post-selected states in the basis of the intermediate measurement. This is done by showing each paradox holds if and only if there is weak-value anomaly for a single, paradox-specific operator, together with the fact that weak-value anomaly requires coherence. This clarifies the role of different notions of nonclassicality in these scenarios, highlighting the required quantitative departures from (strictly) classical explanations provided by incoherent sub-theories of quantum theory.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum-Level Crosstalk Characterization of a 16x16 MEMS Optical Switch for Dynamic Quantum Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24299)
*Persefoni Konteli,Nikolas Makris,Grigoris Anastasiou,Konstantinos Tsimvrakidis,George T. Kanellos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对商用16x16 MEMS光开关进行全对总量子级串扰表征，结合SNSPD用于多用户量子通信，并通过实验数据与理论分析评估串扰对诱骗态BB84 QKD协议的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多用户量子通信网络中，光开关是实现路由的关键器件，但其量子级串扰会引入额外误码率和安全漏洞，目前缺乏对商用MEMS光开关在量子层面的系统串扰表征及其对QKD协议影响的量化分析。

Method: 采用超导纳米线单光子探测器(SNSPD)对商用16x16 MEMS光开关进行全对总量子级串扰测量，建立实验数据与诱骗态BB84 QKD协议安全性分析之间的理论关联模型。

Result: 获得了16x16 MEMS光开关所有端口对的量子级串扰数据，量化了串扰对QKD密钥生成率、量子比特误码率(QBER)及安全性参数的影响。

Conclusion: MEMS光开关的量子级串扰是限制多用户QKD网络性能的关键因素，需在系统设计和协议层面加以缓解。

Abstract: We present an all-to-all quantum-level crosstalk characterization of a commercial 16x16 MEMS optical switch for multi-user quantum communications using SNSPDs, correlating experimental data with a theoretical impact analysis on the decoy-state BB84 QKD protocol.

</details>


### [112] [On the two-copy distillability of Werner states and a new partial trace inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24309)
*Thomas C. Fraser,Felix Huber,Balázs Pozsgay,István Vona*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了《量子信息理论中的五个开放问题》中的问题5，证明了双四维Werner态ϱ(4,-1/2)不是双副本可蒸馏的，并给出了一般性判定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决PRX Quantum上提出的关于双四维Werner态是否双副本可蒸馏的开放问题。

Method: 通过证明一个关于部分迹的范数不等式（对任意秩至多r的矩阵成立），结合Costa Rico关于该不等式与双副本不可蒸馏性等价的结果，在r=2时解决问题5。同时推广到一般维度d上的Werner态。

Result: ϱ(4,-1/2)不是双副本可蒸馏的；对于任意d≥2，ϱ(d,α)是双副本不可蒸馏的当且仅当α≥-1/2；单副本与双副本的可蒸馏区域完全一致。

Conclusion: 回答了开放问题5，揭示了Werner态单副本与双副本可蒸馏区域的完全一致性，并指出该结果由AI辅助完成，可能预示量子信息领域结构性变革。

Abstract: Problem 5 in {\it Five Open Problems in Quantum Information Theory} [PRX Quantum 3, 010101 (2022)], asks whether the two-ququart Werner state $\varrho(4,-\tfrac12)$ is two-copy distillable, where $\varrho(d,α)=(I+αF)/(d^2+αd)$. We answer it in the negative. To this end, we show the following stronger statement: for all $C\in M_{d_1d_2}(\mathbb{C})$ of rank at most $r \le d_1 d_2$, $\mathrm{tr}_1(C)\|_F^2+\|\mathrm{tr}_2(C)\|_F^2 \le r\|C\|_F^2+\frac{1}{r}|\mathrm{tr}(C)|^2$. A result by Costa Rico on the equivalence of this inequality with two-copy undistillability at $r = 2$ then settles Problem 5: $\varrho(4,-\tfrac{1}{2})$ is not two-copy distillable. Furthermore, we show that $\varrho(d,α)$ is two-copy undistillable for every $d\ge2$, if and only if $α\ge-\tfrac{1}{2}$. Thus, the one and two-copy distillability regions of $\varrho(d,α)$ coincide. These results have been found and written up with AI tools, pointing towards a structural change affecting the field of quantum information and computation.

</details>


### [113] [Fast Generation of Metrologically Relevant Fock State Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24318)
*Gonzalo Reina Rivero,Marcel Morillas-Rozas,Alberto López-García,Javier Cerrillo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种快速激光脉冲序列，用于在 trapped ion 中产生非热 Fock 态混合，目标是在标准量子极限以上实现位移计量。通过极化子框架描述，在零失谐且拉比频率匹配阱频率的共振点，实现了强驱动下的选择性布居俘获，无需基态冷却即可超越弱驱动协议速度。通过脉宽重聚焦和 Bloch-Siegert 失谐两种常规校准，抑制了 Lamb-Dicke 参数较大时的残余误差（源于旋转波近似中忽略的反旋蓝边带项）。数值模拟表明，在 η≈0.5 时误差保持在 10% 以下，位移传感 Fisher 信息恢复至比未校正序列高 9 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 trapped ion 位移计量协议在弱驱动极限下速度受限，且通常需要基态冷却，难以快速制备非热 Fock 态混合物以突破标准量子极限。本文旨在提出一种快速制备方案，克服上述限制。

Method: 采用极化子框架描述离子-激光相互作用，确定零失谐且拉比频率匹配阱频率的共振工作点，实现强驱动下的选择性布居俘获。针对大 Lamb-Dicke 参数下的残余保真度损失，识别出反旋蓝边带项为误差源，通过脉宽重聚焦（percent-level）和小补偿 Bloch-Siegert 失谐两种常规校准加以抑制。

Result: 数值模拟显示，经过校准的序列在 η≈0.5 时制备误差仍低于 10%；位移传感 Fisher 信息相较于未校正协议恢复了高达 9 dB，显著恢复了强耦合下丢失的传感性能。

Conclusion: 该快速脉冲序列可在无需基态冷却的条件下高效制备非热 Fock 态混合物，通过简单校准在大 Lamb-Dicke 参数范围内保持低错误率，实现了超越标准量子极限的位移计量能力提升。

Abstract: We propose a fast laser pulse sequence for the generation of non-thermal Fock state mixtures of the motion of a trapped ion, targeted at displacement metrology beyond the standard quantum limit. Using a polaron-frame description of the ion-laser interaction, we identify a resonant operating point-zero detuning and a Rabi frequency matching the trap frequency-at which selective population trapping survives strong driving, enabling preparation speeds beyond the weak-driving limit of previous protocols without requiring ground-state cooling. We trace the residual infidelity at large Lamb-Dicke parameter $η$ to a single coherent process, the counter-rotating blue-sideband term neglected in the rotating-wave approximation, and show that it is suppressed by two routine calibrations: a percent-level refocusing of the pulse duration and a small compensating Bloch-Siegert detuning. Numerical simulations of the full sequence show that this refinement keeps the preparation error at or below the $10\%$ level up to $η\approx0.5$ and restores the displacement-sensing Fisher information that the uncorrected protocol loses at strong coupling, recovering up to 9 dB relative to the nominal sequence.

</details>


### [114] [Electron Shuttle Waiting Times for Electric Field Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24350)
*Alberto Mayorgas,Alberto López-García,Enamul Haque,Javier Cerrillo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子电子梭中等待时间统计在电场传感中的应用，发现从隧穿到穿梭的交叉区域表现出增强的涨落和参数灵敏度，导致Fisher信息显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用量子电子梭中的等待时间统计（而非仅稳态量子Fisher信息）来更灵敏地感知电场（即电子-机械耦合强度）。

Method: 在强库仑阻塞和高偏压条件下，采用马尔可夫量子主方程分析单能级量子梭的稳态相空间动力学和等待时间分布；通过经典Fisher信息评估等待时间对电场的计量灵敏度，并与稳态的量子Fisher信息进行比较。

Result: 从随机隧穿到机械辅助穿梭的交叉区域，涨落增强且参数灵敏度提高，Fisher信息出现显著增强；计量响应与平均等待时间、方差和Fano因子相关。

Conclusion: 等待时间统计在电场传感中优于稳态量子Fisher信息，交叉区域是提升计量灵敏度的关键工作点。

Abstract: We explore the use of waiting-time statistics in a quantum electron shuttle for electric-field sensing. Electron shuttles convert nanomechanical motion into charge transport, showing a noise-broadened crossover between stochastic tunneling and mechanically assisted charge transfer. This allows investigation of how transport fluctuations encode electromechanical parameters. Using a single-level quantum shuttle in strong-Coulomb-blockade and high-bias regimes with a Markovian quantum master equation, we analyze stationary dynamics in phase space and waiting time distributions. By estimating the electromechanical coupling, proportional to the electric field, we evaluate the classical Fisher information in waiting times and compare it with the quantum Fisher information of the stationary state. We relate the metrological response to mean waiting time, variance, and Fano factor. Our results show that the crossover from tunneling to shuttling is characterized by enhanced fluctuations and increased parameter sensitivity, leading to a pronounced enhancement of the Fisher information.

</details>


### [115] [Multivariate Time Series Forecasting with Adaptive Non-Local Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24399)
*Yu-Ting Lee,Huan-Hsin Tseng,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出MTSF-ANO模型，通过自适应非局部观测器增强变分量子电路，在多元时间序列预测中优于固定局部测量方法，在ETT数据集上20个设置中17个排名前二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子神经网络用于多元时间序列预测时依赖固定局部测量，限制了模型表达力，需要更灵活的观测机制。

Method: 将变分量子电路与自适应非局部观测器(ANO)集成，构建混合模型MTSF-ANO，通过可学习的非局部观测替代固定局部测量。

Result: 在四个ETT数据集上，MTSF-ANO在20个设置中有17个MSE排名第一或第二，相比最强基线在ETTh1上提升高达20%，在所有设置中均优于或匹配固定局部观测基线。

Conclusion: 自适应非局部观测器是量子时间序列预测的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting (MTSF) predicts future values of multiple variables from historical data. While quantum neural networks have been increasingly applied to this task, they typically rely on fixed local measurements, which restrict their expressivity. We propose MTSF-ANO, a simple hybrid model for MTSF that integrates variational quantum circuits with adaptive non-local observables (ANO). On the four ETT datasets, MTSF-ANO ranks first or second in MSE in 17 of 20 settings, improving over the strongest baseline by up to 20% on ETTh1, and outperforms or matches its fixed local observable counterpart across all settings. Our ablations show how the quantum circuit design and ANO non-locality affect performance. These results suggest that ANO is a promising direction for quantum time series forecasting.

</details>


### [116] [A Bogoliubov-ratio framework for quantum-information diagnostics of time-dependent two-mode Boson Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24404)
*Shi-Cheng Liu,Lei-Hua Liu,Bichu Li,Yuebing Zhou,Hai-Qing Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Bogoliubov比率λ_k(η)的紧凑统一框架，用于诊断时变双模玻色子系统的量子信息。通过将状态动力学简化为单个复Riccati方程，并利用λ_k的模平方确定约化密度矩阵谱，该框架无需逐模型对角化即可计算纯度、线性熵、Rényi-2熵和von Neumann熵。在宇宙学扰动和啁啾脉冲光学参量放大器两个非平稳系统中的演示验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时变双模玻色子系统量子信息诊断方法需要针对每个具体模型重构和对角化约化密度矩阵，过程繁琐且缺乏通用性。作者旨在建立一个模型无关的统一诊断框架，将模型依赖的驱动协议与普适量子信息度量清晰分离。

Method: 利用Bogoliubov比率λ_k(η)=β_k(η)/α_k(η)将广义时变二次双模哈密顿量的状态动力学简化为单个复Riccati方程。通过证明约化密度矩阵的谱完全由|λ_k|^2决定，推导出纯度、线性熵、Rényi-2熵和von Neumann熵的显式模型无关公式，无需使用耦合压缩参数(r_k,φ_k)进行逐模型计算。

Result: 在宇宙学原初扰动背景下，该框架阐明了背景诱导的相位旋转和频率软化如何调控压缩增长和状态混合度；在光学域中，捕捉了由有限泵浦持续时间和频率啁啾导致的延迟启动、压缩累积抑制以及晚期熵饱和现象。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将模型依赖的驱动协议与普适量子信息度量干净地因子化，为广泛参数驱动二次玻色子系统提供了一种高效、标准化的诊断工具。

Abstract: We present a compact and unified framework for quantum-information diagnostics of time-dependent two-mode bosonic systems based on the Bogoliubov ratio $λ_k(η) \equiv β_k(η)/α_k(η)$. For a general time-dependent quadratic two-mode Hamiltonian, the state dynamics is exactly reduced to a single complex Riccati equation for $λ_k$. Upon tracing out one partner mode, the spectrum of the one-mode reduced density matrix is determined entirely by the squared magnitude $q_k(η) = \vert{}λ_k(η)\vert{}^2$. Consequently, we could construct the explicit, model-independent formula for the for the reduced-state purity, linear entropy, Rényi-2 entropy, and von Neumann entropy without reconstructing and diagonalizing the reduced density matrix on a model-by-model basis using coupled squeezing parameters ($r_k, φ_k$). We demonstrate the utility of this framework in two distinct non-stationary setups: primordial cosmological perturbations and a chirped-pulse nondegenerate optical parametric amplifier. In the cosmological context, our formulation clarifies how background-induced phase rotation and frequency softening regulate squeezing growth and state mixedness; in the optical domain, it captures the delayed onset, suppression of squeezing accumulation, and late-time entropy saturation induced by finite pump duration and frequency chirp. By cleanly factorizing model-dependent driving protocols from universal information-theoretic metrics, this framework offers an efficient, standardized diagnostic tool for a broad class of parametrically driven quadratic bosonic systems.

</details>


### [117] [Floquet time-convolutionless master equation for non-Markovian driven quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24406)
*Pietro Follia,Heinz-Peter Breuer,Bassano Vacchini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文结合Floquet理论和时间卷积无投影算符技术，推导出适用于周期驱动开放量子系统的Floquet时间卷积无主方程，该方程在二阶耦合强度下精确处理周期驱动且不进行马尔可夫近似，能够描述强非马尔可夫效应，并通过驱动自旋-玻色子模型展示了准能级交叉导致的耗散解耦机制和非马尔可夫性尖峰特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放量子系统处理方法通常难以同时满足以下需求：精确处理时间周期驱动、在弱耦合条件下超越马尔可夫近似、且能够描述强非马尔可夫效应。需要发展一种能够兼顾这些方面的理论框架。

Method: 将Floquet理论与时间卷积无投影算符技术相结合，在二阶耦合强度下推导出时间局域量子主方程（Floquet时间卷积无主方程），该方程不进行马尔可夫近似。通过驱动自旋-玻色子模型进行数值验证。

Result: 该主方程能够描述强非马尔可夫效应，在马尔可夫极限下可恢复为Floquet-Lindblad主方程。发现了非马尔可夫性度量作为驱动振幅函数的尖锐峰值特征，这些峰值源于准能级交叉导致的耗散解耦机制，形成准退相干保护子空间。

Conclusion: Floquet时间卷积无主方程为研究周期驱动开放量子系统中的非马尔可夫动力学提供了有效工具，揭示了准能级交叉与耗散解耦机制之间的深层联系，对理解驱动耗散系统中的记忆效应具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of open quantum systems driven by an external time-periodic force. Combining Floquet theory and the time-convolutionless projection operator technique we derive a time-local quantum master equation which exactly takes into account the periodic driving, while treating the system-environment interaction within second order in the coupling strength without performing the Markov approximation. The resulting equation of motion for the reduced density matrix is called Floquet time-convolutionless master equation. Employing the example of the driven spin-boson system, we demonstrate that this master equation is capable of describing strong non-Markovian effects, while yielding the Floquet-Lindblad master equation in the Markovian limit. A characteristic feature of memory effects in such driven dissipative systems is the emergence of sharp peaks of the trace-distance based non-Markovianity measure as a function of the driving amplitude, which can be traced back to quasienergy crossings leading to almost decoherence-protected subspaces through a quasienergy-induced dissipative decoupling mechanism.

</details>


### [118] [QuantumFCS.jl: Efficient Full-Counting Statistics for Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24429)
*Aaron Daniel,Marcelo Janovitch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 介绍了一个名为 QuantumFCS.jl 的开源 Julia 包，用于高效灵活地计算量子输运、量子光学和开放量子系统中的全计数统计（FCS），通过递归累积量算法和多种求解器后端，实现对粒子流、电流和热流的统一处理，并在两个典型物理系统中展示了其性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全计数统计（FCS）是表征量子输运、量子光学和开放量子系统中电流涨落的系统性框架，但目前缺乏高效、灵活且易于使用的数值工具。

Method: 开发了 QuantumFCS.jl 开源 Julia 包，通过监控跳跃算子和权重来定义电流，实现粒子流、电流和热流的统一工作流；采用递归累积量算法，并支持稠密、稀疏和迭代求解器后端。

Result: 在驱动-耗散 Jaynes-Cummings 系统的光子计数（接近 blockade-breakdown 相变）和非线性电路 QED 热引擎的热流涨落两个示例中，展示了高阶累积量如何揭示亮/暗发射间歇性、反聚束现象，并提供了热力学不确定关系探针；与现有 FCS 实现的基准测试显示显著加速。

Conclusion: QuantumFCS.jl 提供了一个高效灵活的 FCS 计算工具，特别适用于直接稠密方法难以处理的模型，并在多个物理系统中验证了其实用性和性能优势。

Abstract: Full-counting statistics (FCS) provides a systematic framework for characterising current fluctuations in quantum transport, quantum optics, and open quantum systems. We introduce QuantumFCS.jl, an open-source Julia package for efficient and flexible numerical FCS calculations. The package defines currents through monitored jump operators and weights, allowing particle, electric, and heat currents to be treated within the same workflow. It implements a recursive cumulant algorithm with dense, sparse, and iterative solver backends, making it suitable for models that are challenging for direct dense methods. We demonstrate the package on photon counting in a driven-dissipative Jaynes-Cummings system near the blockade-breakdown phase transition and on heat-current fluctuations in a non-linear circuit-QED heat engine. These examples show how higher cumulants reveal intermittency between bright and dim emission, antibunching, and provide probes of thermodynamic uncertainty relations. Benchmarks against an existing FCS implementation show substantial speed-ups.

</details>


### [119] [Finite-Precision Algebraic Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24445)
*Abbas Edalat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出区间代数量子场论(IAQFT)，用"量子包裹"(弱*开凸状态区域)代替精确状态，在有限精度下重构了代数量子场论的主要结构，包括Reeh-Schlieder性质、Haag定理、模理论、KMS条件、Unruh效应等，并证明真空关联和Bell不等式违背在有限精度下依然保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将区间量子力学(IQM)的有限精度方法推广到代数量子场论(AQFT)，解决精确量子状态在实际观测中无法获取的问题，建立更符合操作现实的有限精度量子场论框架。

Method: 以"量子包裹"(状态空间中的弱*开凸区域)为基本对象，发展包裹约化和测量更新规则，建立有限维信息压缩结果，并通过兼容包裹网实现局域性。将AQFT的核心结构(Reeh-Schlieder性质、Haag定理、模理论、KMS条件、Unruh效应)用包裹理论重新表述。

Result: (1)类空真空关联和严格Bell违背在有限精度下依然存在；(2)格点近似与包裹框架兼容；(3)从极限包裹的几何中恢复了Murray-von Neumann因子分类及Type II₁因子中的迹与投影等价性。

Conclusion: IAQFT为相对论性量子理论的操作、模和算子代数结构提供了一个统一的有限精度表述框架。

Abstract: Recent work introduced Interval Quantum Mechanics (IQM), a finite-precision framework in which physical information is represented by sets of possible states rather than exact states. We extend this approach to algebraic quantum field theory (AQFT) by introducing Interval Algebraic Quantum Field Theory (IAQFT), whose basic objects are quantum parcels: weak* open convex regions of state space encoding finite-precision information obtained from finitely many local observations.
  We develop parcel reduction and measurement update, establish finite-dimensional information-contraction results, and formulate locality through compatible parcel nets. Major structural features of AQFT are recast in parcel-theoretic terms, including the Reeh--Schlieder property, Haag's theorem, modular theory, the KMS condition, and the Unruh effect. We show that spacelike vacuum correlations and strict Bell violations persist under finite precision, and that lattice approximations are compatible with the parcel framework. Finally, we recover the Murray--von Neumann classification of factors together with the trace and projection equivalence in Type~$\mathrm{II}_1$ factors from the geometry of limiting parcels. IAQFT thus provides a unified finite-precision formulation of the operational, modular, and operator-algebraic structures of relativistic quantum theory.

</details>


### [120] [The bare necessities of a physically reasonable mathematical model for quantum theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24474)
*Gerd Niestegge*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子理论数学形式化的物理基础只需三个基本特征：转移概率、连续可逆动力学过程的存在性及其在底层空间上的传递性。由此导出的模型包括原子JBW因子（有限维下退化为Jordan矩阵代数）以及一个例外模型——例外李群E6在其上传递作用。在例外模型中，量子理论的许多熟悉特征（如测量后态的一般存在性）会丧失。最后讨论了开放问题和数学结构的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子理论数学形式化的物理基础至今仍是个谜，作者试图从最少的物理合理假设出发，构建能够重现量子理论的数学模型。

Method: 假定三个基本特征：（1）转移概率；（2-3）连续可逆动力学过程存在且在其底层空间上传递作用。在此框架下考察原子JBW因子（包括原子von Neumann因子和有限维Jordan矩阵代数）以及例外李群E6的传递作用模型。

Result: 原子JBW因子类模型保有量子理论的主要特征；例外E6模型虽满足三个基本特征，但失去了量子理论的许多熟悉性质（如测量后态的一般存在性）。

Conclusion: 三个基本特征能导出量子理论的核心数学结构（JBW因子），但也会产生例外模型（E6），后者缺失了量子理论的某些关键特征。该问题及数学结构的完全分类仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: The physical foundation of the mathematical formalism of quantum theory is still an iffy mystery. Here it is presumed that a physically reasonable mathematical model needs only three basic features. The first one are the transition probabilities, which are so typical of quantum theory. The other two constitute a variation of the postulate that continuous reversible dynamical processes exist and act transitively on the underlying space. One class of mathematical models with these features arises from the atomic JBW factors, which include the atomic von Neumann factors and become identical with the Jordan matrix algebras, when the dimension is finite. A further model is known, on which the exceptional Lie group E6 acts transitively. Interestingly, E6 is sometimes considered a candidate for internal symmetries in particle physics, but many familiar features of quantum theory get lost in this case (particularly the general existence of post-measurement states). The paper concludes with some open issues, concerning this problem and the classification of the mathematical structures with the three features.

</details>


### [121] [Two-copy nondistillability of Werner states: sharp partial-trace inequalities and finite-copy extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24479)
*Kishor Bharti,Rishikesh Gajjala,Tobias Haug*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了Werner态的双拷贝可蒸馏性问题，证明了Werner态 ρ_α 是双拷贝可蒸馏的当且仅当 α < -1/2，其中包括解决Horodecki等人的问题5。对于任意有限拷贝数k，给出了三个等价表述。作者还证明了若干多拷贝扩展结果，包括显式常数 γ_k 使得 α ≥ -γ_k 时Werner态是k拷贝不可蒸馏的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Werner态的双拷贝可蒸馏性问题，特别是回答Horodecki, Rudnicki, 和 Życzkowski 提出的问题5（即两ququart Werner态 ρ^{(4)}_{-1/2} 是否双拷贝可蒸馏）。该问题与量子纠缠理论中的可蒸馏性这一基本问题密切相关。

Method: 核心方法是证明了一个尖锐的无维数不等式：对于任意秩不超过2的算子，其两个部分迹的Hilbert-Schmidt范数平方之和不超过其Hilbert-Schmidt范数平方的两倍加上其迹模平方的一半。利用这一矩阵不等式推导Werner态的双拷贝可蒸馏性判据。对于多拷贝情形，给出了三个等价的剩余问题表述，并证明了算子不等式层级 H_k(ψ) ≥ 0。通过张量积分解和构造显式常数 γ_k 实现多拷贝扩展。

Result: Werner态 ρ_α 双拷贝可蒸馏当且仅当 α < -1/2。两ququart态 ρ^{(4)}_{-1/2} 是双拷贝不可蒸馏的。对于多拷贝，给出了三个等价表述；证明了对于任意张量积分解，若其可能的秩二因子至多支撑在两个拷贝的块上，则端点不等式成立；构造了显式常数 γ_k > 0 使得 α ≥ -γ_k 时Werner态在任意维度上都是k拷贝不可蒸馏的。

Conclusion: 完整解决了Werner态的双拷贝可蒸馏性问题，并为多拷贝情形提供了若干实质性进展和精确的剩余问题表述。证明了该问题的核心归结为端点 α = -1/2 处一个关于秩不超过二算子的部分迹形式非负性条件。

Abstract: We solve the two-copy distillability problem for Werner states in every local dimension. Our main matrix result is a sharp, dimension-free inequality: for every rank-at-most-two operator, the sum of the squared Hilbert--Schmidt norms of its two partial traces is bounded by twice its squared Hilbert--Schmidt norm plus one half of the squared modulus of its trace. This implies that a Werner state $ρ_α$ is two-copy distillable if and only if $α<-1/2$. In particular, the two-ququart state $ρ^{(4)}_{-1/2}$ is two-copy undistillable, resolving Problem 5 of Horodecki, Rudnicki, and Życzkowski. For an arbitrary finite number $k$ of copies, we give three exact formulations of the remaining problem. At the endpoint $α=-1/2$, undistillability is equivalent to nonnegativity of the endpoint partial-trace form on every rank-at-most-two operator. We also derive an equivalent hierarchy of operator inequalities $H_k(ψ)\succeq0$ for pure states with a maximally mixed qubit marginal. The two-copy proof does not formally induct, because partial trace can increase rank and $2$-positivity is not generally preserved by tensor products. We prove two rigorous many-copy extensions. First, the quadratic form factorizes exactly on tensor-factorized witnesses; for any such decomposition, the endpoint inequality holds if its possible rank-two factor is supported on a block containing at most two copies. Second, we construct explicit constants $γ_k>0$ such that $α\ge-γ_k$ implies $k$-copy undistillability in every dimension. The initial proofs were generated by ChatGPT 5.6 Sol; the authors have verified and rewritten them to enhance readability and provide additional context.

</details>


### [122] [A Bohmian version of a 2-state quantum system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24480)
*Matthew Dickau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 作者构建了一个2态量子系统的玻姆版本，其中系统除了量子态外，还具有确定的物理态，该物理态在量子态的引导下随机变化。作者论证该过程产生的概率是良定义的，反驳了Gillespie(1994)关于相关模型的论断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳Gillespie(1994)关于"量子力学无法被表述为马尔可夫过程"的论断，证明在玻姆框架下2态量子系统的随机过程概率是良定义的。

Method: 构建一个2态量子系统的玻姆版本，在标准量子态之外引入一个确定的物理态，该物理态在量子态的引导下发生随机变化，从而形成一种随机过程。

Result: 论证了该玻姆型随机过程产生的概率是良定义的，即不存在Gillespie所指出的问题。

Conclusion: 玻姆力学框架下的2态量子系统可以成功地被表述为具有良定义概率的随机过程，反驳了Gillespie的反对观点。

Abstract: I construct a Bohmian version of a 2-state quantum system, where in addition to the quantum state, the system is characterized by a definite physical state which changes stochastically under the guidance of the quantum state. I argue that the probabilities produced by this process are well-defined, contrary to claims in "Why quantum mechanics cannot be formulated as a Markov process" by D. Gillespie (1994) about a related model.

</details>


### [123] [Bulk spectra and the non-Hermitian skin effect in systems with long-range couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24496)
*Jonathan Sturm,Pia Kreß,Adriana Pálffy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了计算晶格模型能带结构的两种方法——无限晶格和周期边界条件，发现对于长程耦合系统，两者结果显著不同。在一维量子发射器链（自由空间和波导耦合）中，只有周期边界条件能得到物理和数学上稳健的结果，而无限链方法存在收敛问题，无法合理解释波导系统中的非厄米趋肤效应。波导系统还展现了非星形广义布里渊区和零缠绕数的强局域化本征态等异常现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对长程耦合系统中无限晶格近似与周期边界条件这两种能带计算方法可能不等价的问题，澄清哪种方法更可靠，特别是在非厄米趋肤效应等新奇物理现象中的适用性。

Method: 以自由空间和波导耦合的一维量子发射器链为模型系统，分别采用无限晶格方法和周期边界条件计算能带结构，并对两者进行比较分析，特别关注非厄米趋肤效应的表现。

Result: 周期边界条件在长程耦合系统中给出物理和数学上稳健的结果；无限链方法存在收敛问题，无法满意解释波导系统中的非厄米趋肤效应。波导系统还发现了非星形广义布里渊区和零缠绕数的强局域化本征态。

Conclusion: 对于长程耦合系统，周期边界条件优于无限晶格方法，是计算能带结构的更可靠选择。

Abstract: To achieve translational symmetry for the computation of the band structure of a lattice model, one can either consider an infinite lattice or impose periodic boundary conditions. While in sys- tems with short-range couplings these two approaches are equivalent, we show that for long-range couplings one can obtain considerably different results. We compare the two methods on the basis of one-dimensional quantum emitter chains both in free space and when coupled to a waveguide. The latter system allows for asymmetric couplings enabling the non-Hermitian skin effect, which we analyze using the two different band-structure calculation methods. We find that only periodic boundary conditions lead to physically and mathematically robust results, while the infinite-chain approach entails convergence issues and is unable to satisfyingly explain the emergence of the non- Hermitian skin effect in the waveguide system. In addition, the waveguide system yields unusual findings like a non-star shaped generalized Brillouin zone and strongly localized eigenstates with zero winding number.

</details>


### [124] [Thermal Quantum Correlations in Coupled Andreev Spin Qubits: Interplay of Superconducting Phase and Spin-Orbit Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24521)
*Mohammad Reza Pourkarimi,Mina Shiri,Mehdi Monemi,Vadim Ohanyan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由有效Andreev自旋量子比特哈密顿量描述的两个耦合超导自旋量子比特系统中的热量子关联，使用局部量子Fisher信息（LQFI）和局部量子不确定性（LQU）作为量化指标，分析了超导相位差、隧穿振幅、自旋轨道耦合和温度对非经典性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究混合超导量子器件中热量子关联的微观机制，理解超导相位、自旋轨道相互作用和隧穿如何影响量子资源的保护。

Method: 利用LQFI和LQU作为量子关联量化器，推导热密度矩阵的解析表达式，分析超导相位差、隧穿振幅、自旋轨道耦合和温度等参数对量子关联的影响，并结合能谱分析解释增强机制。

Result: 量子关联随温度升高单调递减；更强的隧穿和自旋轨道耦合可显著增强其鲁棒性；超导相位通过调制有效交换耦合引入周期性行为，产生相长和相消干涉；LQFI始终大于LQU；量子关联增强与基态和第一激发态之间的能隙增大密切相关。

Conclusion: 揭示了Andreev自旋量子比特中热量子关联的微观机制，强调了相位工程、自旋轨道相互作用和隧穿在保护混合超导量子器件中量子资源的重要作用。

Abstract: We investigate thermal quantum correlations in a system of two coupled superconducting spin qubits described by an effective Andreev spin-qubit Hamiltonian in the presence of spin-orbit interaction. Using Local Quantum Fisher Information (LQFI) and Local Quantum Uncertainty (LQU) as quantum-correlation quantifiers, we analyze the effects of the superconducting phase difference, tunneling amplitude, spin-orbit coupling, and temperature on the nonclassical properties of the system. Analytical expressions for the thermal density matrix are obtained and employed to evaluate both quantities. Our results show that quantum correlations decrease monotonically with increasing temperature, while stronger tunneling and spin-orbit interaction significantly enhance their robustness. Moreover, the superconducting phase introduces a pronounced periodic behavior through the modulation of the effective exchange couplings, leading to constructive and destructive interference regimes that strongly influence the correlations. By analyzing the energy spectrum of the effective Hamiltonian, we demonstrate that the enhancement of quantum correlations is closely associated with an increased energy gap between the ground and first excited states, which suppresses thermal excitations and stabilizes the correlated ground state. Furthermore, LQFI is consistently larger than LQU throughout the investigated parameter space, reflecting its higher sensitivity to quantum fluctuations and local parameter estimation. These findings reveal the microscopic mechanism governing thermal quantum correlations in Andreev spin qubits and highlight the important roles of phase engineering, spin-orbit interaction, and tunneling in protecting quantum resources in hybrid superconducting quantum devices.

</details>


### [125] [Entanglement Distillation and Swapping Scheduling in Quantum Repeaters with Noisy Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24557)
*Siddharth Chander,Xinan Chen,Allen Zang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子存储器退相干对纠缠蒸馏和纠缠交换操作时机选择的影响。在单跳场景中，低相干时间时丢弃旧态策略保真度最高，高相干时间时延迟蒸馏策略保真度和加权相干信息最优；在两跳中继链中，延迟蒸馏至时间窗口末尾的策略表现最佳，而丢弃最旧态策略从未达到正加权相干信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠缠蒸馏和纠缠交换研究假设完美量子存储器，但近期的量子网络受限于有限相干时间的量子存储器，导致操作时机和顺序的复杂选择。本文旨在阐明存储器退相干所诱导的基本权衡关系。

Method: 针对单跳（最小一跳设置）进行解析分析，比较"尽早蒸馏"、"尽晚蒸馏"和"丢弃旧纠缠态"三种策略；针对两跳中继链使用蒙特卡洛模拟，评估多种操作时序策略。

Result: 低相干时间时丢弃旧态策略保真度最高；高相干时间时延迟蒸馏至末尾策略保真度和加权相干信息最优，但成功概率较低。在两跳场景中，延迟蒸馏至时间窗口末尾的策略加权相干信息最高，Discard-Oldest-then-Swap在所有测试区域均未达到正加权相干信息。

Conclusion: 退相干重塑了量子网络中的最优操作时序，研究结果为更大规模架构的链路级原理提供了指导性见解。

Abstract: Entanglement distillation and entanglement swapping have been extensively studied assuming perfect quantum memories. However, near-term quantum networks will be fundamentally limited by quantum memories with a finite coherence time, resulting in complex choices for the timing and ordering of these operations. In this work, we study entanglement distillation and entanglement swapping at the level of the elementary building blocks of noisy quantum repeater networks, with the goal of elucidating the fundamental tradeoffs induced by memory decoherence. First, we focus on a minimal one-hop setting, where we analytically compare ``distill-as-soon-as-possible'' and ``distill-as-late-as-possible'' strategies against a baseline strategy that simply discards the older entangled state. We find that in the low memory coherence time regime, discarding the older entangled state achieves higher expected output fidelity, while in the high coherence time regime, delaying distillation until the end achieves the highest expected output fidelity and, over most of the deadline range, the highest weighted coherent information, at the expense of a lower success probability. We then extend our analysis to two-hop repeater chains using Monte Carlo simulation. In this setting, we find that the highest weighted coherent information is achieved by strategies that defer distillation to the end of the time window, with \textsf{Distill-ALAP-then-Swap-ALAP} and \textsf{Swap-ASAP-then-Distill-ALAP} leading at different operating points, while \textsf{Discard-Oldest-then-Swap} never reaches positive weighted coherent information in any regime tested. Together, these results clarify how decoherence reshapes the optimal operation timing in quantum networks and provide instructive insights into the link-level principles that govern larger-scale architectures.

</details>


### [126] [Unravelling time-resolved Interparticle Coulombic Decay: From spectral formation to decay lifetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24597)
*Alexander V. Riegel,Max K. Humm,Jan-Thore Kahle,Elke Fasshauer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了时间分辨电子衰变谱的解析描述，纳入了解离核动力学，并应用于氖二聚体中的粒子间库仑衰变（ICD）。研究发现干涉效应的重要作用，揭示了衰变信号包含exp(-t/τ)和exp[-t/(2τ)]两种贡献，而传统单指数拟合会导致寿命严重偏差。将理论模型应用于实验数据，将ICD寿命从150(50) fs修正为73 fs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子衰变过程中核运动如何影响可观测的时间分辨谱信号尚不清楚，传统方法提取的衰变寿命可能存在系统性偏差，需要发展更精确的理论描述。

Method: 扩展时间分辨电子衰变谱的解析理论，纳入解离核动力学；将理论应用于氖二聚体ICD过程；推导时间依赖积分信号的解析表达式；建立拟合模型并应用于实验数据。

Result: 发现时间积分ICD信号包含exp(-t/τ)和exp[-t/(2τ)]两项贡献；传统单指数拟合会得到严重偏离真实值的寿命；将ICD寿命从之前提取的150(50) fs修正为73 fs，与理论预测范围一致。

Conclusion: 由于时间依赖结构在电子衰变过程中具有普适性，该发现对从时间分辨衰变谱中提取寿命具有广泛意义，远超ICD范畴。

Abstract: Electronic decay processes provide a fascinating window into correlated electronic rearrangements occurring on ultrafast timescales. Following these dynamics in real time has become increasingly accessible experimentally, but extracting the underlying electronic dynamics from measured spectra requires understanding how nuclear motion shapes the observable signal. Here, we extend an analytical description of time-resolved electronic decay spectra to include dissociative nuclear dynamics and apply it to Interparticle Coulombic Decay (ICD) in the neon dimer. The resulting spectra reproduce the experimentally observed spectral shape and reveal an unexpectedly important role of interference between pathways involving different vibronic resonance states. We further establish a clear connection between the temporal build-up of the spectral structure and the nuclear wavepacket dynamics in the decaying electronic state. Most strikingly, our analytical expressions reveal that the time-dependent integrated ICD signal contains contributions proportional to both $\exp(-t/τ)$ and $\exp[-t/(2τ)]$. Nevertheless, a conventional mono-exponential fit can describe the temporal signal remarkably well while yielding a decay lifetime that differs substantially from the underlying value. Applying the theoretically derived fitting model to experimental data for the neon dimer yields an ICD lifetime $τ$ of 73 fs, rather than the previously extracted 150(50) fs, placing the experimental value within the range of previous theoretical predictions. Since the underlying temporal structure is common to electronic decay processes, our findings have implications for extracting lifetimes from time-resolved decay spectra well beyond ICD.

</details>


### [127] [Experimental Protocol Fingerprinting in Quantum Networks via Physical Layer Side Channel Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24624)
*Lance Young,Contessa Wilburn,Carrie Houston,Blaine Keyton,Marwan Elawady,Mohamed Shaban,Muhammad Ismail*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实验验证了量子通信协议可通过物理层侧信道（如单光子检测统计和光功率测量）被被动推断，在30:70光抽头配置下协议识别准确率达96%，且该观测过程不破坏量子纠缠特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典系统中侧信道分析可泄露敏感信息，但量子通信协议是否面临类似风险尚未被探索。本文旨在探究不同量子通信协议是否可通过被动侧信道观测被区分，揭示潜在安全威胁。

Method: 基于偏振纠缠光子链路，对四种代表性协议（纠缠分发、量子门序列、 heralded QKD、量子身份认证）进行实验。通过单光子检测统计和光功率测量收集物理层特征，构建协议指纹，采用数据驱动框架实现协议识别。利用贝尔不等式测量验证观测的非破坏性。

Result: 30:70光抽头配置下协议识别准确率达96%；10:90配置下准确率70-89%。贝尔不等式测量证实采样/抽头过程保持纠缠特性，观测模型具有非破坏性。

Conclusion: 侧信道分析可在不破坏量子关联的情况下暴露协议级信息，为量子网络安全引入新的安全考量，表明量子网络同样面临物理层侧信道泄露风险。

Abstract: Quantum communication is a key enabler of next-generation networks, leveraging quantum entanglement to enable a new class of information exchange. While prior work has focused on the theoretical analysis of communication protocols, their exposure to physical layer side channel analysis remains largely unexplored. In classical systems, side channel analysis has been shown to reveal sensitive information without accessing the underlying data, raising the question of whether similar risks exist in quantum networks. In this work, we investigate whether different quantum communication protocols exhibit distinguishable signatures that can be inferred through passive side channel observations. We consider a threat model in which an observer accesses only a fraction of the optical signal without directly measuring the encoded quantum states. Under this setting, we experimentally examine four representative protocols, namely entanglement distribution, quantum gate sequences, heralded quantum key distribution, and quantum identity authentication, realized on a polarization entangled photon link. Observable physical layer features, including single photon detection statistics and optical power measurements, are collected and used to construct protocol fingerprints. We develop a data-driven framework for protocol identification based on these observations. Our results show that protocol identity can be inferred with accuracy reaching up to 96% under 30:70 sampling configuration/optical tapping, while remaining distinguishable at 10:90 with accuracy ranging from 70-89%. Bell inequality measurements confirm that the sampling/tapping process preserves entanglement, validating the non-destructive nature of the observation model. These findings demonstrate that side channel analysis can expose protocol-level information without disrupting quantum correlations, introducing new security considerations.

</details>


### [128] [Experimental Side Channel Analysis of Protocol Stages in Quantum Identity Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24639)
*Marwan Elawady,Lance Young,Contessa Wilburn,Blaine Keyton,Carrie Houston,Mohamed Shaban,Muhammad Ismail*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实验性地揭示了量子身份认证协议在物理层侧信道攻击下的脆弱性。通过在量子通信测试平台上分光（30%或10%）收集光子到达时序和光功率数据，利用机器学习模型成功推断协议阶段，准确率高达98%（F1分数97%）。该攻击可使恶意中继绕过认证量子比特，提取数据量子比特，导致认证失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络依赖身份认证来防止中间人攻击（恶意中继保留纠缠而非执行交换）。然而现有工作仅分析安全性和中间人攻击检测保证，物理层侧信道分析仍是空白。若攻击者能推断协议阶段，可避开认证量子比特并提取数据量子比特，使认证机制完全失效。

Method: 在量子通信测试平台上，使用分束器分别将30%或10%光信号分给攻击者侧信道观测装置。采集数据包括：单光子探测器记录的到达时序和功率计记录的光功率。从中提取时序动态和信号强度变化的特征，训练机器学习模型，对协议阶段进行分类。

Result: 30%采样下协议阶段分类准确率达98%（F1分数97%），10%采样下准确率达96%（F1分数94%）。表明即使仅窃取10%信号，也能高精度推断协议阶段。

Conclusion: 量子身份认证协议的协议阶段信息可通过物理层侧信道泄露，攻击者据此可绕过认证机制。该漏洞此前被忽视，需要设计鲁棒的抗侧信道推断攻击方案。

Abstract: Quantum networks can enable distributed computing and sensing. To realize these capabilities securely, quantum identity authentication is essential. Without authentication at the quantum layer, malicious repeaters may retain entanglement instead of performing swapping, enabling man-in-the-middle attacks (MitM) between communicating parties. Authentication mitigates this threat by embedding authentication qubits within data qubits at positions and bases based on a secret key shared a priori. While prior work analyzes security and MitM detection guarantees, physical layer side channel analysis remains unexplored. If an attacker infers protocol stages, it can avoid authentication qubits and extract data qubits, rendering authentication ineffective. To this end, we carry out experimental studies using a quantum communication testbed. A beam splitter is used to tap a portion of the optical signal, allowing the observer to collect side channel data without disrupting the quantum state. We evaluate two sampling settings, where 30% or 10% of the signal is diverted. The collected side channel data includes photon arrival timing and optical power data obtained using a single-photon detector and a power meter. Using this dataset, we extract and engineer features that capture both timing dynamics and signal intensity variations. We then train machine learning models to classify protocol stages based solely on side channel observations. Our results show that protocol-stage inference is feasible with high accuracy, reaching 98% (F1-score 97%) at 30% sampling and 96% (F1-score 94%) at 10% sampling. These findings reveal an overlooked vulnerability and highlight the need for robust designs against side channel inference attacks.

</details>


### [129] [Quantum-Limited Symbol-Blind Channel Estimation for Coherent State Discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24644)
*Hongxu Chen,Renzhi Yuan,Haifeng Yao,Mugen Peng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了光子匮乏相干链路中残余色散对时间模式匹配的影响，针对M进制PSK相干态，在已知光谱模式但符号和载波相位未知的情况下，推导了盲联合估计群延迟和二阶色散的量子极限，并提出多输出量子脉冲门与光子数分辨检测方案可达到该极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残余色散会破坏光子匮乏相干链路中的时间模式匹配，从而限制通信性能。本文旨在建立盲联合估计群延迟和二阶色散的量子极限，并设计可达到该极限的实际检测方案。

Method: 理论推导了盲联合估计的量子极限，证明消除公共相位后，极限由中心化生成元的协方差矩阵4N_s C决定。提出了采用多输出量子脉冲门（multi-output quantum pulse gate）结合光子数分辨探测的接收方案。

Result: 盲联合估计群延迟和二阶色散的量子极限为4N_s C；所提方案可局部达到该极限，并且在湍流衰落条件下支持低于标准量子极限的接收性能。

Conclusion: 该方案能在光子匮乏和湍流衰落环境下实现超越标准量子极限的接收，为相干光通信中的色散补偿与信号检测提供了量子最优方案。

Abstract: Residual dispersion breaks temporal-mode matching in photon-starved coherent links. For equiprobable $M$-ary PSK coherent states in a known spectral mode, with unknown symbols and carrier phase, we establish the quantum limit for blind joint estimation of group delay and second-order dispersion: after eliminating the common phase, it is $4N_s\mathbf{C}$, set by the covariance of the centered generators alone. A multi-output quantum pulse gate with photon-number-resolving detection locally attains it and supports reception below the standard quantum limit under turbulent fading.

</details>


### [130] [Geometric bounds on multiparameter Heisenberg scaling in optical metrology with limited squeezed resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24657)
*Atmadev Rai,Paolo Facchi,Vincenzo Tamma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多模高斯网络中，使用k个单模压缩态和至少一个相干态作为探针时，量子Fisher信息矩阵中具有海森堡标度灵敏度的独立参数组合的最大数量，给出了上界n_HS ≤ min{p, k(k+3)/2}，并分解为协方差贡献（最多k(k+1)/2个）和一阶矩贡献（最多k个），同时给出了饱和条件及相应无源干涉仪族构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计量学中多参数同时估计是一个核心问题，一个基本问题是给定一定数量的压缩探针，在多模高斯网络中究竟有多少独立的参数组合能够继承海森堡标度。本文旨在回答任意无源线性光学网络中这一问题的答案。

Method: 通过分析量子Fisher信息矩阵的海森堡标度系数秩，将其分解为协方差贡献和一阶矩贡献两个几何上不同的部分，分别导出各自的上界，并构造能够饱和这些边界的无源干涉仪族。

Result: 得到海森堡标度系数秩的上界为n_HS ≤ min{p, k(k+3)/2}，其中协方差贡献最多提供k(k+1)/2个独立参数组合，一阶矩贡献最多提供k个独立参数组合。同时识别了饱和这些边界的条件，并构造了能够饱和边界的无源干涉仪族。

Conclusion: 本文完整回答了在任意无源线性光学网络中，给定k个单模压缩态和至少一个相干态时，最多能够同时估计多少具有海森堡标度灵敏度的独立参数组合，为多参数量子计量学提供了基础性的理论界限。

Abstract: The simultaneous estimation of multiple parameters is a central task in quantum metrology, distributed sensing, and the calibration of large photonic interferometers. A fundamental question is how many independent parameter combinations can inherit Heisenberg scaling from a given number of squeezed probes in a multimode Gaussian network. Here, we answer this question for arbitrary passive linear optical networks. For a $p$-parameter, $M$-channel interferometer probed by $k$ single-mode squeezed states and at least one coherent state in the remaining channels, we show that the rank of the Heisenberg-scaling coefficient of the quantum Fisher information matrix is bounded by $n_{\rm HS}\le \min\{p,k(k+3)/2\}$, which corresponds to the maximum number of independent combinations of parameters that can be estimated with Heisenberg-scaling sensitivity. The bound separates into two geometrically distinct contributions. The covariance contribution of the quantum Fisher information, which describes squeezing-enhanced fluctuations, provides at most $k(k+1)/2$ parameter combinations estimable at Heisenberg-scaling sensitivity, while the first-moment contribution provides at most $k$ additional independent parameter combinations with Heisenberg-scaling sensitivity. We identify the conditions for saturating these bounds and construct a passive family of interferometers that saturates these bounds.

</details>


### [131] [Exact and Fixed-Point Grover Search with Qudits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24658)
*Tanay Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个适用于qudit（多能级量子系统）的Grover搜索统一框架，包括量子门构造、扩散算子、相位匹配技术以及确定性/固定点搜索变体，并给出了布洛赫球可视化分析和适用于异构硬件的电路分解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Grover算法基于qubit（二能级系统）实现，但随着量子平台向qudit（多能级系统）乃至异构qudit体系发展，现有方法无法直接迁移，亟需一个统一的、可适配不同维度qudit的Grover搜索框架。

Method: 提出了统一框架，涵盖：1) 有无辅助qubit的oracle和扩散算子构造；2) 确定性与固定点搜索变体；3) 相位匹配技术分析；4) 显式电路分解；5) 布洛赫球轨迹可视化对比不同相位选择对目标态放大的影响。

Result: 给出了适用于多种硬件平台的显式电路分解方案，并提供了布洛赫球上的直观可视化对比，展示了不同相位选择如何影响目标态的放大效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为qudit处理器上实现Grover搜索提供了灵活、硬件友好的协议，有望降低电路深度、提高成功概率，为利用多能级量子系统的量子计算与传感应用提供了实用工具集。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm provides a quadratic speedup for searching unstructured databases and is traditionally implemented with qubits in Hilbert spaces whose dimensions are powers of two. With the advent of quantum platforms utilizing qudits---quantum systems with more than two levels---there is a need to generalize Grover search to these architectures, including heterogeneous systems with qudits of varying dimensions. Here, we present a unified framework for qudit-based Grover search, detailing the construction of oracles and diffusion operators with and without ancilla qubits and generalizing deterministic and fixed-point search variants that ensure exact or bounded success probabilities. We analyze phase-matching techniques and provide explicit circuit decompositions suitable for diverse hardware platforms. We also compare the corresponding trajectories on the Bloch sphere to provide an intuitive visualization of how the different phase choices amplify the target state. These results facilitate flexible, hardware-oriented protocols for implementing Grover search on qudit processors, potentially reducing circuit depth and enhancing success probabilities, thereby offering a practical toolkit for quantum computation and sensing applications leveraging multilevel quantum systems.

</details>


### [132] [CP-preserving channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24682)
*Indu Bala,Sourav Das,Swapan Rana*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了完全正（CP）矩阵在量子资源理论框架下的性质，提供了小维度下CP保持通道的充要条件，证明了迹距离非负性度量违反强单调性，并给出了两类CPDNN通道是CPCP通道的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应Johnston等人将CP矩阵纳入量子资源理论框架后遗留的问题，包括CP保持通道的刻画条件、资源量化度量的合理性验证，以及CPDNN通道与CPCP通道之间关系的澄清。

Method: 通过理论推导给出小维度下CP保持通道的充要条件；构造显式反例证明迹距离非负性度量违反强单调性；采用替代证明方法证明CPDNN通道到二维系统的CPCP性质；利用幺正性条件证明二维到任意维CPDNN映射的CPCP性质。

Result: 得到了小维度CP保持通道的充要条件（在高维仅为必要条件）；发现了迹距离非负性度量不满足强单调性；证明了任意CPDNN通道Φ:M_n→M_2是CPCP的；证明了任意幺正CPDNN映射Φ:M_2→M_n也是CPCP的。

Conclusion: 本文推进了对CP矩阵量子资源理论的理解，揭示了迹距离度量在资源理论中的局限性，并拓展了CPDNN与CPCP通道之间等价关系的已知边界。

Abstract: Completely positive (CP) matrices are ubiquitous in modern science and technology with applications in optimization, graph theory, and quantum entanglement. Recently, Johnston \emph{et al.} [Linear Algebra and its Applications, 2022] have cast CP matrices into the framework of quantum resource theories, where CP states serve as free states and CP-preserving channels act as free operations. This work addresses several questions raised in their work. Specifically, we provide the necessary and sufficient conditions of CP-preserving channels in small dimensions, which are necessary in higher dimensions, and discuss the resource quantification via the trace distance of non-negativity. By constructing an explicit counterexample, we demonstrate that the trace-distance measure of non-negativity violates strong monotonicity. We also provide an alternative proof that every CPDNN channel $Φ:\MM_n\to \MM_2$ is CPCP. Additionally, we show that any unital CPDNN map $Φ:\MM_2\to \MM_n$ is also CPCP.

</details>


### [133] [Stacking the Deck: Tunable Trainability in Stacked LCUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24686)
*Nikhil Khatri,Stefan Zohren,Gabriel Matos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出堆叠线性酉组合（S-LCU）作为变分量子电路的ansatz，通过层数l实现训练性与经典可模拟性之间的可调平衡。证明自由费米子S-LCU的损失景观方差下界为Ω(1/(n k^{3l}))，经典模拟代价O(k^{2l} n³)，量子门复杂度仅O(lkn²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子电路在训练性与量子优势之间存在根本矛盾：足够表达能力强以避免经典有效模拟的ansatz易出现贫瘠高原，而能排除贫瘠高原的结构通常又使电路可经典模拟。需要一种可调谐的权衡方案。

Method: 提出堆叠线性酉组合（S-LCU）变分ansatz，基于费米子高斯酉的线性组合。通过图分析技术，推导了损失景观方差下界，并分析了经典模拟复杂度与量子门复杂度。

Result: 自由费米子S-LCU的损失景观方差下界为Ω(1/(n k^{3l}))，经典模拟代价O(k^{2l} n³)，量子门复杂度O(lkn²)。层数l作为单一旋钮可调节计算复杂度与代价集中率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: S-LCU为实践者提供了一种系统化构建ansatz的方法，可根据具体应用和硬件需求，在复杂度与训练性之间进行最优权衡。

Abstract: Variational quantum circuits have been central to many proposed near-term applications of quantum computing, but a growing body of evidence suggests that trainability and quantum advantage are fundamentally at odds: ansätze expressive enough to resist efficient classical simulation tend to exhibit barren plateaus, while structures that provably rule out barren plateaus typically render them classically simulable. We propose a stacked linear combination of unitaries (S-LCU) as a variational ansatz which provides a tunable trade-off between barren plateaus and classical simulability. Using a diagrammatic analysis, we bound the loss-landscape variance of the Free Fermion S-LCU, whose elements are fermionic Gaussian unitaries. We prove a variance lower bound of $Ω(1/(n k^{3l}))$, with a simulation cost of $O(k^{2l} n^3)$ using the best known classical algorithm, compared to a quantum gate complexity of only $O(lkn^2)$. The number of layers $l$ serves as a single dial that trades computational complexity against the rate of cost concentration. This offers practitioners a systematic method for constructing ansätze with a complexity-trainability trade-off that best suits their application and hardware.

</details>


### [134] [Sharp continuity of quantum conditional entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24687)
*Mario Berta,Pablo Costa Rico,Gereon Kossmann,Ludovico Lami,Julius A. Zeiss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子条件熵的尖锐一致连续性界：当两个双粒子态迹距离≤δ且d=dim A时，最优维度模连续函数为h₂(δ)+δlog(d²-1)（δ≤1-d⁻²）和2log d（δ>1-d⁻²），在dim B≥d时对任意δ∈[0,1]紧致。证明关键思路由ChatGPT 5.6 Sol协助开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给出量子条件熵在迹距离下的尖锐（tight）一致连续性界，填补该领域最优维数相关模连续函数的空白。

Method: 借鉴并适配Alhejji & Smith在经典情形下的紧致证明思路，由ChatGPT 5.6 Sol协助开发关键证明思想，采用与经典概念不同的量子方法。

Result: 得到了量子条件熵关于迹距离δ和子系统维数d的尖锐一致连续性界：当δ≤1-d⁻²时为h₂(δ)+δlog(d²-1)，当δ>1-d⁻²时为2log d；且当dim B≥d时该界对每个δ∈[0,1]都是紧的。

Conclusion: 成功建立了量子条件熵的最优一致连续性界，证明了其紧致性，展示了AI辅助数学证明的潜力。

Abstract: We prove the sharp uniform continuity bound for quantum conditional entropy. If two bipartite states are at trace distance at most $δ$ and $d=\dim A$, the optimal dimension-only modulus of continuity is $h_2(δ)+δ\log(d^2-1)$ up to $δ=1-d^{-2}$ and $2\log d$ thereafter, where $h_2$ denotes the binary entropy. When $\dim B\ge d$, this bound is tight for every $δ\in[0,1]$. The key proof idea was developed with the assistance of ChatGPT 5.6 Sol, building on and adapting the tight classical proof of Alhejji \& Smith [IEEE ISIT (2020)], which follows a conceptually different approach.

</details>


### [135] [Quantum Incapacity beyond No-Cloning and PPT Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24693)
*Chengkai Zhu,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个显式的qutrit信道，其私有容量和量子容量均为零，但该信道既不是反降级的，也不是部分转置正（PPT）的。这解决了量子信息理论中两个长期悬而未决的问题：零量子容量能否出现在PPT和反降级类之外，以及反降级性是否是迫使私有容量消失的唯一非平凡机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子信息理论中两个长期开放问题：1) 零量子容量是否可能出现在PPT信道和反降级信道之外；2) 反降级性是否是导致私有容量为零的唯一非平凡机制。此前学界普遍认为PPT束缚纠缠和反降级（无克隆机制）是容量为零的两大来源，但尚无信道能同时排除这两类机制而仍具零容量。

Method: 构造了一个显式的qutrit信道 Λ_{A→B}(X)= (1/2)X + (1/4)(Tr(X)𝟙_B - X^T)。利用一个非正、保伴随的有符号提升（signed lift），其加权缺陷具有精确的秩三完全正分解。结合Bogoliubov-Kubo-Mori/相对熵比较结果以及完全少噪声张量积化结果，证明在所有张量幂次和任意有限维量子参考系统下，互补输出在相对熵意义上支配接收者输出。进而证明优化私有信息和相干信息在每个块长度下均为零，从而P(Λ)=Q(Λ)=0。

Result: 成功构造了一个qutrit信道，其P(Λ)=Q(Λ)=0，但该信道既不是PPT信道也不是反降级信道。这证明零量子容量可以出现在PPT和反降级类之外，且反降级性不是迫使私有容量为零的唯一非平凡机制。

Conclusion: 该信道实现了超越标准PPT束缚纠缠机制和无克隆机制的精确量子与私有无能力（quantum and private incapacity），为量子信道容量理论提供了新的结构类型，表明存在更丰富的机制导致容量消失。

Abstract: We show an explicit qutrit channel whose private and quantum capacities both vanish, although it is neither antidegradable nor positive under partial transposition (PPT). This resolves two longstanding open problems in quantum information theory: whether zero quantum capacity can occur outside the PPT and antidegradable classes, and whether antidegradability is the only nontrivial mechanism that forces the private capacity to vanish. For qutrit systems $A$ and $B$, the channel is \[ Λ_{A\to B}(X)= \frac{1}{2}X+\frac{1}{4}\left(\operatorname{Tr}(X)\mathbb{1}_{B}-X^{\mathsf T} \right). \] We prove that, for every tensor power and every finite-dimensional quantum reference system, the complementary output dominates the receiver output in relative entropy. Consequently, we show that the optimized private and coherent information vanish at every blocklength, and hence $P(Λ)=Q(Λ)=0$. The proof uses a nonpositive, adjoint-preserving signed lift whose weighted defect admits an exact rank-three completely positive factorization. Applying established Bogoliubov--Kubo--Mori/relative-entropy comparison results and complete less-noisy tensorization results then yields the stated order at every tensor power, leading to zero quantum and private capacities. Meanwhile, we show that the channel is neither PPT nor antidegradable. The channel therefore realizes exact quantum and private incapacity beyond the standard PPT-based bound-entanglement mechanism and the no-cloning mechanism.

</details>


### [136] [Almost all pure entangled states enable unbounded nonlocality sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24700)
*Anna Steffinlongo,Nicola D'Alessandro,Martin J. Renner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了Hardy悖论与顺序双粒子场景中非局域性共享之间的联系，证明几乎所有纯纠缠态（除最大纠缠态外）在辅助少量局域辅助系统后，使用投影测量即可实现任意多对观测者之间的CHSH不等式连续违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Hardy悖论与非局域性共享之间的深层联系，寻找在顺序测量场景中实现任意多对观测者之间非局域性共享的通用方案。

Method: 通过建立Hardy悖论与非局域性共享的理论联系，证明存在Hardy悖论的两输入两输出关联可实现连续CHSH不等式违反；利用几乎所有纯纠缠态（除最大纠缠态外）都能产生Hardy关联这一特性，结合局域辅助系统扩展协议适用范围。

Result: 任意呈现Hardy悖论的关联都允许在顺序测量场景中实现任意多对观测者之间的CHSH不等式违反；几乎所有纯纠缠态（辅以局域辅助系统）均可实现这一非局域性共享，且仅需投影测量。

Conclusion: Hardy悖论与非局域性共享存在本质联系，该发现为量子非局域性的基础理解和量子信息处理应用提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We establish a connection between Hardy's paradox and nonlocality sharing in sequential bipartite scenarios, where each subsystem is measured in turn by a chain of observers. We show that any correlations exhibiting a Hardy paradox in the two-input two-output scenario enable sequential violations of the CHSH inequality between arbitrarily many pairs of observers, using only projective measurements and the assistance of a small local ancilla system. Since almost all pure entangled states, with the only exception of the maximally entangled one, admit Hardy correlations, our protocol applies generically: almost all pure entangled states, if assisted by a local ancilla, allow for nonlocality sharing between arbitrarily many observer pairs using only projective measurements.

</details>


### [137] [How Many Shots Does It Take? A Noise-Aware Quantum Resource Allocation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24704)
*Prateek P. Kulkarni,Sumit K. Mandal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种闭式解析表达式来确定量子计算机上算法可靠执行所需的最优射击次数，以及一种在量子电路不同分区之间分配固定射击预算的理论方法，可将射击次数减少约58%，能耗降低高达62%，并将总误差降低高达73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法执行需要大量重复执行（shots）才能获得可靠结果，当前实践缺乏确定最优射击次数的理论指导，导致资源浪费和执行成本过高。

Method: 提出闭式解析表达式计算最优射击次数；提出理论驱动的射击预算分配技术，在量子电路不同分区之间分配固定射击预算以最小化总误差。

Result: 相比当前实践，射击次数减少约58%，能耗降低高达62%；相比传统方法，总误差降低高达73%。

Conclusion: 所提出的解析模型和分配技术能够显著提升量子算法执行的资源效率和可靠性。

Abstract: Any algorithm execution on quantum computers requires several repeated and costly executions (known as shots) to obtain reliable results. In this work, we propose a closed-form accurate analytical expression to determine optimal number of shots required for reliable execution of any algorithm on a quantum computer. We also present a theoretically grounded technique to distribute fixed shot budget across different partitions in a quantum circuit minimizing the total error. Our proposed analytical model helps to reduce the shots associated with reliable execution of quantum algorithms by about 58\% compared to current practice, in turn reducing the energy consumption by upto 62\%. Furthermore, our proposed optimal shot allocation technique across different partitions reduces total error by up to 73\% compared to conventional approaches.

</details>


### [138] [Sample complexity of quantum resource testing via one-shot quantum blurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24712)
*Dmitry Grinko,Ludovico Lami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子资源测试的首个有限副本（finite-n）界，解决了广义量子Stein引理只能处理渐近情形、无法提供有限资源保证的局限。主要结果包括：(a) 资源正则化Rényi相对熵的收敛性，解决了Fang/Hayashi（IEEE ToIT, 2026）提出的开放问题；(b) 非对称资源测试的首个样本复杂度界：对于任意固定的假阳性错误概率，假阴性错误概率≤δ所需副本数为n=O(log(1/δ)/D^∞(ρ∥F))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有广义量子Stein引理给出了量子资源测试的渐近刻画，但该结果是渐近的，无法提供有限资源下的保证，使得实际应用意义不明确。因此，亟需建立有限副本下的严格界，以指导实际量子信息处理中的资源测试与操控。

Method: 通过建立量子资源测试的首个有限n严格界，利用资源理论的正则化Rényi相对熵等工具，推导出达到给定性能所需副本数的显式估计。具体依赖于对假阳性和假阴性错误概率的有限样本分析。

Result: (a) 证明了资源正则化Rényi相对熵的收敛性；(b) 给出了非对称资源测试的样本复杂度上界：n=O(log(1/δ)/D^∞(ρ∥F))，其中δ→0时假阴性错误概率趋于零。

Conclusion: 本文填补了量子资源测试从渐近理论到有限样本分析的空白，为实际量子信息处理中资源测试与操控提供了可操作的有限资源保证，并解决了领域内的重要开放问题。

Abstract: Quantum resource testing is a fundamental primitive of quantum information processing, profoundly connected to resource manipulation. Its goal is to discriminate $n$ copies of a given resourceful state $ρ$ from all free (i.e., resourceless) states; key instances for applications are entanglement testing and quantum magic testing. The asymptotic characterisation relies on the recently proven generalised quantum Stein's lemma, which establishes the rate of decay of the false negative error probability for a fixed false positive error probability. This result, however, is intrinsically asymptotic and thus can provide no finite-resource guarantees, which makes its practical implications unclear. Here, we establish the first rigorous finite-$n$ bounds on quantum resource testing and hence quantum resource manipulation, providing explicit estimates on the number of copies needed to achieve a prescribed performance. As notable consequences, we obtain (a) the convergence of the regularised Rényi relative entropies of a resource, which settles the important open problem from [Fang/Hayashi, IEEE ToIT 72:6, 2026]; and (b) the first sample-complexity bound for asymmetric resource testing: for any fixed false positive error probability, a false negative error probability of at most $δ$ can be achieved with $n=O\left(\frac{\log(1/δ)}{D^\infty(ρ\|F)}\right)$ copies of $ρ$, in the limit where $δ\to 0$.

</details>


### [139] [Efficient LLM-Generated Shuttling Compilers for Complex Trapped-Ion Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24714)
*Fabian Kreppel,Reza Salkhordeh,Ferdinand Schmidt-Kaler,André Brinkmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次利用单个前沿大语言模型（Claude Opus 4.7）从书面规范生成并迭代优化离子阱量子计算中离子穿梭编译器的完整Python代码，依次针对线性分段阱、带结的阱和通用连接阱图架构生成编译器，并在基准测试中相比手工编译器将穿梭时间步数最多减少76%，表明未经修改的LLM可在几天内生成可工作的竞争性编译器，将开发时间从数月缩短至数天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子阱量子计算机依赖穿梭编译器将算法转换为离子运动序列，但传统手工开发编译器耗时数月，且不同架构需要重新设计。探索能否利用前沿大语言模型直接从书面规范自动生成高效穿梭编译器，以大幅缩短开发周期。

Method: 使用Claude Opus 4.7从书面规范生成Python代码，采用迭代优化策略：先为线性分段阱生成编译器，再扩展至带结架构，最后推广到通用连接阱图。更通用的编译器通过种子代码继承自前一代。用第二款前沿LLM（Claude Fable 5）重复完整生成与评估以验证可复现性。

Result: 对于线性分段阱，穿梭时间步数减少高达76%；对于带结架构，减少高达39%。在通用连接架构中，高连接度、多结的架构相比走廊式架构减少一个数量级的穿梭步数。Claude Fable 5的编译器在最大规模电路上更频繁地超越手工编译器。

Conclusion: 未经修改的前沿LLM能够从书面规范生成正确、可工作且具有竞争力的穿梭编译器，无需额外的手工算法工程，可将新架构的开发时间从数月缩短至数天。

Abstract: Trapped-ion quantum computers rely on shuttling compilers, which cast an input algorithm into a sequence of ion-qubit movements within a given architecture. We present the first study in which a single frontier large language model (LLM), Claude Opus 4.7, generates and iteratively refines the full Python code of shuttling compilers from written specifications. We start with a compiler for (i) a linear segmented trap, extend it to (ii) a trap with junctions, and finally achieve efficient compilation for (iii) a broad class of connected trap graphs. The compilers for the more general cases are seeded with code from the previous ones. We benchmark the LLM-generated compilers against state-of-the-art hand-crafted ones using a common suite of quantum circuits. The number of shuttling timesteps is reduced by up to 76% for (i) and up to 39% for (ii). For the broad case (iii) of freely connected architectures, we find large variations in the required number of shuttling timesteps, depending on the connectivity. A densely connected, junction-rich architecture yields an order-of-magnitude reduction in shuttling timesteps compared to a corridor-like one. Repeating the complete generation and evaluation with a second frontier LLM, Claude Fable 5, reproduces these findings, with the Fable 5 compilers surpassing the hand-crafted ones more often on the largest circuits. Our results show that an unmodified frontier LLM can produce working, correct, and competitive shuttling compilers without additional manual algorithmic engineering, thus reducing the development time for new architectures from several months to a few days.

</details>


### [140] [Coincidence free certification and quantification of spatial entanglement with stimulated parametric down conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24718)
*M. G. Damaceno,G. H. dos Santos,N. Rubiano da Silva,S. P. Walborn,P. H. Souto Ribeiro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用受激发射替代自发参量下转换中的双光子符合计数，通过种子光注入信号模并测量受激闲频光强度，仅用强度测量即可认证连续空间自由度中的纠缠特性，实验验证了基于方差纠缠判据、操控判据和Fedorov比的方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠表征依赖双光子符合计数，在空间自由度测量中面临对准困难、计数耗时等问题，亟需更简便的经典强度测量方案来认证空间纠缠。

Method: 将受激发射方法应用于连续横向空间自由度：用明亮经典光束种子注入信号模，测量受激闲频光强度分布，替代双光子符合计数；基于强度测量数据构造方差纠缠判据、方差操控判据和Fedorov比来认证空间纠缠。

Result: 实验证明仅通过强度测量即可有效认证空间纠缠，三个判据（方差纠缠判据、方差操控判据、Fedorov比）均成功验证了纠缠的存在。

Conclusion: 基于受激发射的强度测量方法为空间纠缠研究提供了一种实用工具，特别适用于对准困难和光子计数耗时的实验条件。

Abstract: Using stimulated emission, a photon pair source can be characterized by seeding the signal mode with a bright classical beam and measuring the stimulated idler field, thus replacing two-photon coincidence counting with classical intensity detection. We apply this approach to the continuous transverse spatial degrees of freedom of a down conversion source and show that it is possible to certify spatial entanglement with only intensity measurements. We demonstrate this capability through variance-based entanglement and steering witnesses, as well as the Fedorov ratio. This method is useful for studying entanglement properties of photon pair sources in conditions where alignment and photon counting measurements are difficult and time-consuming.

</details>


### [141] [Scalable Variational Quantum Optimization via Pauli Correlation Encoding: Application to Large-Scale Power Demand Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24722)
*Takuya Yoshioka,Keita Sasada,Riku Usuki,Yuichiro Nakano,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于泡利关联编码(PCE)的可扩展变分框架，用于电力需求组合优化。通过泡利关联算符的期望值表示二元变量，实现连续松弛和少量量子比特的紧凑编码。两阶段混合公式(时间平均+时间分辨)在m=18至10,296规模上实现近最优性能(归一化成本差距约10^-4)，并在离子阱量子处理器上展示了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法在组合优化中受限于量子比特资源不足，难以编码大规模问题。现有方法缺乏一种既能高效利用量子比特又能保持求解质量的编码方案。

Method: 1) 泡利关联编码(PCE)：通过泡利关联算符的期望值表示二元变量，利用量子态的多体关联实现连续松弛和紧凑编码；2) 两阶段混合公式：时间平均问题初始化→时间分辨优化。

Result: 在m=18至10,296规模上，归一化成本差距约10^-4；性能受连续松弛与离散化之间的权衡支配；在离子阱量子处理器上展现了噪声和有限采样下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PCE建立了一种物理动机明确、量子比特高效的大规模组合优化框架，有效解决了变分量子算法在量子比特资源受限下的可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms offer a promising route to combinatorial optimization, but their applicability is limited by the challenge of encoding large-scale problems within restricted qubit resources. In this work, we introduce a scalable variational framework based on Pauli correlation encoding (PCE) and apply it to electric power demand portfolio optimization. Binary variables are represented through expectation values of Pauli correlation operators, which encode multi-body correlations of the quantum state and provide a continuous relaxation enabling compact representations with few qubits. We further propose a two-stage hybrid formulation, in which a time-averaged problem provides initialization for a time-resolved optimization. Numerical simulations demonstrate near-optimal performance across problem sizes ranging from $m$=18 to 10,296, with normalized cost gaps on the order of $10^{-4}$ relative to solutions with certified optimality. We show that the performance is governed by the interplay between continuous relaxation and discretization: the effective resolution of the correlator representation determines how reliably improvements in the continuous loss translate into better discrete solutions, with larger systems exhibiting more consistent behavior. Finally, we demonstrate robustness on a trapped-ion quantum processor, where high-quality solutions are obtained despite noise and finite sampling. These results establish PCE as a physically motivated and qubit-efficient framework for large-scale combinatorial optimization.

</details>


### [142] [Pulse engineering via projection of response functions at infinite nonlinear order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24725)
*Lia Kley,Ludwig Mathey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PEPRino的超参数无梯度量子最优控制算法，利用响应理论框架高效求解多量子比特系统的高保真控制协议，在2-qubit和3-qubit QFT任务中相较于CRAB算法展现出更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子最优控制问题中存在高维控制景观和超参数敏感性的挑战，现有优化算法（如CRAB）性能严重依赖超参数选择，影响收敛效率和控制保真度。

Method: 提出PEPRino算法，采用响应理论框架，通过无限阶响应函数确定控制景观，并利用第一、二阶响应函数的重求和实现高效评估，实现超参数无梯度的控制协议优化。

Result: 在2-qubit和3-qubit系统上实现QFT最优控制，与CRAB+Nelder-Mead算法对比，在迭代步数和计算时间上均表现出更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: PEPRino作为一种超参数无梯度的量子最优控制方法，能够有效生成多量子比特系统的高保真控制协议，具有更优的收敛性能。

Abstract: Optimal control problems arise in a wide range of scientific disciplines, but the corresponding optimization algorithms often display a strong dependence on hyperparameters that significantly influence performance and convergence. For the optimal implementation of quantum algorithms, these challenges are further amplified by high-dimensional control landscapes and the need for high-fidelity operations. Here, we propose an algorithm for optimal control problems in quantum computing to efficiently generate high-fidelity control protocols for multi-qubit systems in a hyperparameter and gradient free manner. The method, referred to as Pulse Engineering via Projection of response functions at infinite nonlinear order (PEPRino), leverages the framework of response theory to navigate the control landscape to find high-fidelity implementations. This is achieved by determining the control landscape via response functions to infinite order, efficiently evaluated by resummation in terms of the first and second order response function. To demonstrate the approach, we apply it to quantum systems consisting of two and three qubits for the optimal implementation of the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT). We benchmark the proposed algorithm against the Chopped Random Basis (CRAB) algorithm utilizing the Nelder-Mead method, focusing on the 2-qubit scenario. The results indicate faster convergence regarding iteration steps and computational time, highlighting the advantages of our approach.

</details>


### [143] [Classical simulation and model concentration in passive linear optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24728)
*Léo Monbroussou,Hugo Thomas,Hela Mhiri,Zoë Holmes,Elham Kashefi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用表示论框架系统研究了无源线性光学电路中随机电路矩的浓度现象，揭示了期望值浓度由输入态和可观测量在不可约表示中的投影错位主导，并建立了与经典可模拟性之间的联系。研究识别出可训练的、可高效经典模拟的观测量类别，同时发现Fock态输入和特定观测量可避免指数浓度且保留多项式信号分量，但大部分信号仍可经典模拟，残余部分虽非指数压制但可通过截断实现多项式误差的经典近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中，无源线性光学是具有近期量子优势潜力的受限模型，但量子优势常与贫瘠高原（barren plateaus）和经典可模拟性存在关联。在玻色子系统中，这种权衡关系尚未被充分探索。本文旨在系统刻画无源线性光学随机电路中期望值的浓度行为，并厘清其与经典可模拟性的界限。

Method: 基于近期发展的随机无源线性光学电路矩的表示论框架，通过将期望值投影到酉群的不可约表示中并分析其渐近标度，来刻画粒子数守恒可观测量家族在随机电路下的浓度特性。

Result: 1) 浓度由输入态与可观测量在不可约表示中投影的错位程度决定，统一了玻色子场景下的广义纠缠和局域性概念；2) 识别出可高效经典模拟的可训练观测量类别；3) Fock态输入和某些观测量可避免指数浓度并保留多项式信号分量，但大部分信号仍可经典模拟，残余信号虽非指数压制但可通过截断实现多项式精度的近似模拟。

Conclusion: 本文为无源线性光学中浓度现象与经典可模拟性建立了系统化的表示论框架，虽然目前分离只是部分的，但该框架为寻找能同时避免指数浓度且超出已知经典模拟方法的参数区域提供了系统化的搜索路径。
