Abstract: Passive linear optics is a restricted model of quantum computation, with complexity-theoretic evidence of quantum advantage for sampling tasks and low losses that make it attractive for near-term algorithms. In qubit architectures, a body of work has revealed a close connection between barren plateaus and classical simulability. Whether an analogous tradeoff exists for bosonic systems remains largely unexplored. Building on a recently developed representation-theoretic framework for moments of random passive linear-optical circuits, we characterize the concentration of expectation values for relevant families of particle-number-preserving observables by evaluating their projections into irreducible representations of the unitary group and analyzing their asymptotic scaling. We show that concentration is governed by the misalignment of the projections into irreducible representations of the input state and the observable, giving a unified representation-theoretic interpretation of generalized entanglement and locality in the bosonic setting. We further relate these concentration properties to existing classical simulation techniques, identifying broad classes of trainable observables that admit efficient classical simulation. Conversely, we identify Fock-state inputs and observables that appear to evade exponential concentration while retaining a polynomially large signal component not accessible to known efficient classical simulation methods. The separation is only partial: most of the signal remains classically tractable, and the residual part, while not exponentially suppressed, is small enough that a truncation serves as a classical surrogate with polynomially small error. Our framework nonetheless provides a systematic route for searching for regimes that unambiguously combine the absence of exponential concentration and lies beyond known efficient classical simulation methods.

</details>


### [144] [Quantum simulacra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24739)
*L. F. Alves da Silva,M. H. Y. Moussa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"量子拟像"概念：通过改变量子系统的度量（非标准L²度量），可以重新定义可观测量及其测量方式，从而模拟出与哈密顿量所规定的不同的微观实在。这为解释最近观察到的非纠缠光子违反贝尔不等式现象提供了新视角，并提出结合正算子值测量与后选择子方案来实现度量重定义可观测量的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量在量子力学中占据核心地位，但标准L²度量下的观测框架可能限制了对量子现象的理解和量子操作的实现。作者试图通过引入非标准度量来扩展量子力学的描述能力，以解释现有理论难以理解的实验现象（如非纠缠光子违反贝尔不等式），并拓展量子操作的实现范围。

Method: （1）理论分析：将厄米或非厄米量子系统置于非L²度量下分析，重新定义可观测量集和实验安排；（2）应用解释：用该框架解释非纠缠光子违反贝尔不等式的实验现象；（3）实现方案：提出结合正算子值测量（POVM）与后选择子方案的技术路线，用于实现度量重定义可观测量的测量。

Result: （1）发现量子上下文性和微观实在具有度量依赖性；（2）在适当度量下进行的测量可以模拟出与哈密顿量规定不同的微观实在；（3）量子拟像提供了实现超出L²度量范围量子操作的途径；（4）为2025年报道的非纠缠光子违反贝尔不等式现象提供了理论解释——在L²度量下可分离的光子，在新的度量框架下变得纠缠。

Conclusion: 量子拟像概念揭示了度量选择在量子测量和微观实在描述中的根本性作用：改变度量重新定义了可观测量及其测量方式，使得量子测量从连接经典-量子动力学的桥梁扩展为模拟不同微观实在的工具。尽管实现度量重定义可观测量需要付出代价，但POVM结合后选择的技术方案为解决这一挑战提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Here we analyze the creation of quantum simulacra: phenomena that emerge from treating a Hermitian or non-Hermitian quantum system in metrics other than the standard $L^{2}$. Changing the metric redefines the set of system observables and thus the experimental arrangement for their measurement, making quantum contextuality and microscopic reality metric-dependent. The simulacra therefore consist, on the one hand, of a resizing of the status of quantum measurement, which has always occupied a central role in quantum mechanics: beyond the connection between quantum and classical dynamics, measurements performed in an appropriate metric can emulate a microscopic reality distinct from that prescribed by the Hamiltonian. On the other hand, simulacra provide a route to implementing quantum operations that lie beyond the reach of the $L^2$ metric. Quantum simulacra offer, as an example, an explanation for the recent observation of the violation of Bell inequalities with unentangled photons [Sci. Adv. \textbf{11}, eadr1794 (2025)]: photons that are separable in $L^2$ metric, become entangled when analyzed within a new metric framework. Simulacrum comes at the cost of implementing measurements of the metric-redefined observables; to address this challenge, we propose a scheme combining positive operator-valued measures with postselected subensembles.

</details>

### [145] [Fourth-order closure obstruction and chiral nonlocality in circular kinetic magnetotransport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22730)
*P. Shubham Parashar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在应力级别截断角动量层次结构会遗漏高阶费米面谐波的反作用，产生四阶闭包障碍项κ₄q⁴，并分析了其梯度展开条件、零场/磁场下的行为以及与精确Bessel极点-零点阶梯的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应力级截断角动量层次会忽略高阶费米面谐波的反作用，作者旨在揭示这种截断导致的缺失项（四阶闭包障碍）及其物理后果。

Method: 采用圆形二维费米面模型，分析角动量层次中流变（streaming）导致的角动量变化序列1→2→3→2→1，推导出电流本征值Λ(q)的四阶项κ₄=ν²/γ₃；通过梯度展开控制条件νq²/γ₃«1分析收敛性；在零场和磁场下分别研究闭包障碍的行为。

Result: 发现截断引入四阶项Λ(q)=γ₁+νq²-κ₄q⁴+⋯；圆对称性将相同系数带入径向双拉普拉斯算子；零场下正碰撞率排除实波数极点和响应零点；磁场使系数手性化并产生霍尔符号反转；碰撞极限下完整层次呈现贝塞尔极点-零点阶梯。

Conclusion: 应力级截断的闭包障碍产生可控的低梯度系数与几何/场依赖的有限波数完成项之间的分离，为理解流体动力学截断的局限性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Closing an angular moment hierarchy at the stress level omits a definite back-action from higher Fermi-surface harmonics. For a circular two-dimensional Fermi surface, streaming changes angular momentum by one, so the shortest omitted sequence, $1\!\to\!2\!\to\!3\!\to\!2\!\to\!1$, adds a fourth-order term to the current eigenvalue, $Λ(q)=γ_1+νq^2-κ_4q^4+\cdots$, with $ν=v_F^2/(4γ_2)$ and $κ_4=ν^2/γ_3$. We call this missing operator term the fourth-order closure obstruction. Its gradient expansion is controlled when $νq^2/γ_3\ll1$. Circular symmetry carries the same coefficient into a radial bi-Laplacian within each conserved angular-momentum block, and retaining $m=3$ exactly, without a gradient expansion, amplifies higher radial modes monotonically. At zero field, positive collision rates exclude real-wave-number poles and response zeros; an equal-rate tail gives a square-root completion. A magnetic field makes the coefficient chiral, produces a Hall sign reversal, and enhances it when the $m=3$ harmonic is long lived. In the collisionless high-field limit, the complete hierarchy becomes a Bessel pole--zero ladder, while finite closures form rational approximants to it. The result separates a controlled low-gradient coefficient from its geometry- and field-dependent finite-wave-number completion.

</details>


### [146] [Theory of Andreev reflection spectroscopy with anisotropic spin-dependent scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22747)
*Zhiyue Li,Guoping Zhang,Tingyong Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种包含自旋依赖各向异性散射的推广模型，通过扩展BTK和CTC理论，统一描述了从正常金属到半金属的Andreev反射谱，揭示了界面各向异性对自旋极化电流和超导间隙测量的关键影响，并在外延Co薄膜实验中验证了模型有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Andreev反射谱理论假设各向同性界面散射，忽略了异质结中普遍存在的各向异性，导致材料自旋极化率和超导间隙的测量结果被错误解读。

Method: 引入自旋向上和自旋向下电子的不同界面势垒，扩展BTK和CTC形式论；通过修正边界条件求解Bogoliubov-de Gennes方程，导出包含透射概率判断函数的电流公式。

Result: 非磁性金属中界面各向异性可通过透射自旋过滤产生可观自旋极化电流，抑制Andreev反射并降低子隙电导；正极化铁磁体中各向异性非线性调制极化率，先增强后抑制Andreev反射；负极化材料呈现逆光谱，可通过电导比较明确判断极化符号。

Conclusion: 该模型改进了Andreev谱的解析精度，支持利用非磁性平台构建抗干扰的自旋源，对磁阻器件和超导量子技术有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Spintronic technologies require efficient generation and control of spin-polarized currents. Conventional ferromagnet-based methods suffer from sensitivity to external magnetic fields. Andreev reflection spectroscopy is vital for measuring spin polarization and superconducting gaps, yet prevailing theories assume isotropic interface scattering. This neglects ubiquitous anisotropy in heterostructures, causing misinterpretation of material properties. To resolve this, we develop a generalized model incorporating spin-dependent anisotropic scattering. Introducing distinct interface barriers for spin-up and spin-down electrons extends both the Blonder-Tinkham-Klapwijk formalism and the Chen-Tesanovic-Chien extension. This unified framework describes transport from normal metals to half-metals. Solving the Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations with modified boundary conditions yields current formulae with a transmission probability judgment function identifying dominant spin channels. In non-magnetic metals, interfacial anisotropy generates sizable spin-polarized currents via transmission spin filtering, suppressing Andreev reflection and reducing sub-gap conductance. For positively polarized ferromagnets, anisotropy nonlinearly modulates polarization, enhancing Andreev reflection to a threshold before suppression. Negatively polarized materials exhibit inverse spectra, enabling unambiguous polarization sign determination via conductance comparisons. Epitaxial Co film measurements validate the model, resolving subtle anisotropies. This refines Andreev spectrum interpretation and supports interference-resistant spin sources using non-magnetic platforms, benefiting magnetoresistive devices and superconducting quantum technologies.

</details>


### [147] [Radiative Spin Caloritronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22845)
*Philippe Ben-Abdallah*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文预测了非互易磁光多体系统中的自旋热霍尔效应，其中纵向辐射热流可产生横向光子自旋角动量积累，并建立了该效应与逆自旋热霍尔效应的Onsager-Casimir互易关系，提出了热-自旋品质因子来量化辐射自旋-热转换效率，为光子自旋热电子学建立了完整的热力学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非互易光子系统中热流与光子自旋角动量之间的耦合机制，建立光子自旋热电子学的完整热力学理论框架，为自旋控制的热辐射和非互易光子热器件奠定概念基础。

Method: 通过理论分析非互易磁光多体系统中的辐射热传递和自旋角动量输运，推导出自旋热霍尔效应及其逆效应的Onsager-Casimir互易关系，并结合热力学第二定律导出热-自旋耦合的基本界限及品质因子。

Result: 发现了非互易磁光系统中的自旋热霍尔效应（纵向热流产生横向自旋积累）及其逆效应；建立了它们作为Onsager-Casimir互易对的关系；导出了热力学第二定律对自旋-热耦合的基本约束；定义了热-自旋品质因子。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了光子自旋热电子学的完整热力学框架，为自旋控制的热辐射和非互易光子热器件提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We predict the spin thermal Hall effect in nonreciprocal magneto-optical many-body systems, in which a longitudinal radiative heat current generates a transverse accumulation of the spin angular momentum carried by thermal photons. We show that this effect and the inverse spin thermal Hall effect constitute an Onsager-Casimir reciprocal pair, thereby establishing heat and photon spin as coupled transport channels in nonreciprocal photonic systems. The second law of thermodynamics imposes fundamental bounds on the spin-heat coupling, leading to a thermal-spin figure of merit that quantifies the efficiency of radiative spin-heat conversion. Our results establish a complete thermodynamic framework for photon spin caloritronics and lay the conceptual foundations for spin-controlled thermal radiation and nonreciprocal photonic thermal devices.

</details>


### [148] [Spectral Topology and Non-Bloch Band Theory for Domain-Wall Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22976)
*Mingtao Xu,Rui Wang,Tian-Shu Deng,Wei Yi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维非厄米系统在域壁构型（环形排列不同域）中的谱拓扑，揭示了本征态在界面处的局域化源于相邻域之间谱缠绕数的差异，并通过扩展Ronkin函数形式得到了广义布里渊区条件。除了传统的开边界皮肤模外，还发现了一种新型的行波状皮肤模，其构建涉及所有域，且具有有限通量谱缠绕数，表现出边界敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非厄米系统中域壁构型下的谱拓扑行为，特别是本征态局域化与谱缠绕数之间的关系，以及广义布里渊区在复杂动量空间中的条件。

Method: 通过域壁构型（环形几何中排列不同域）研究一维非厄米模型，利用Ronkin函数形式扩展得到广义布里渊区条件，分析皮肤模的局域化来源。

Result: 本征态局域化源于相邻域间谱缠绕数的差异；发现了两种皮肤模：传统的开边界条件下的驻波型皮肤模，以及新型的行波型皮肤模，后者涉及所有域且具有有限通量谱缠绕数。

Conclusion: 两种皮肤模在空间分布和广义布里渊区条件上存在可区分的差异，行波型模的有限通量谱缠绕数指示其边界敏感性。

Abstract: We study the spectral topology of one-dimensional non-Hermitian models in a domain-wall configuration, where different domains are arranged in a ring geometry. While eigenstates can localize near an interface under the non-Hermitian skin effect, we show that the localization of an eigenstate originates from the difference in the spectral winding numbers, with respect to the corresponding eigenenergy, between the two adjacent domains. We then obtain the conditions for the generalized Brillouin zone (GBZ) in the complex momentum space, by extending the Ronkin-function formalism to the domain-wall configuration. In addition to the conventional skin modes that correspond to standing waves on individual domains under the open boundary condition, a unique type of traveling-wave-like skin modes emerges, whose construction involves all domains. Besides their difference in the spatial profiles, these two types of modes obey distinct GBZ conditions, making them differentiable on the GBZ. Interestingly, the traveling-wave-like modes further carry a finite flux spectral winding number, indicating their boundary sensitivity.

</details>


### [149] [Janus-induced atomic reconstruction amplifies twist-angle modulation of interlayer thermal transport in moiré bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23103)
*Bin Xu,Rulei Guo,Jie Sun,Hiroo Suzuki,Takuma Yoshida,Ichiro Nakaya,Koki Sawasaki,Yasuhiko Hayashi,Shohei Chiashi,Tomoki Machida,Junichiro Shiomi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了Janus结构（MoSSe/MoS₂）中镜面对称破缺对二维莫尔双层热传导的影响，发现原子重构在近公度 regime 下显著改变层间耦合，导致热导率在小于5°扭转角时出现异常下降，降幅比传统MoS₂双层大一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Janus诱导的镜面对称破缺能否通过调控原子重构来有效调制二维莫尔双层中的层间声子热传输。

Method: 对比研究MoSSe/MoS₂（Janus结构）与MoS₂/MoS₂双层体系，通过分析原子重构、层间耦合以及扭转角依赖的热导率变化，揭示镜面对称破缺的作用机制。

Result: 1) MoSSe/MoS₂中本征面外偶极矩导致无摩擦界面，降低晶格变形能，促进形成局部扭曲的非周期莫尔图案；2) 这些特征弱化了层间耦合，抑制界面声子传输；3) 热导率呈现异常强的扭转角依赖性，在小扭转角出现明显最小值，降幅比扭转MoS₂双层大近一个数量级。

Conclusion: 层间热传输可通过原子重构进行调控，Janus诱导的镜面对称破缺是促进二维莫尔结构中声子工程的有效途径。

Abstract: In two-dimensional moiré bilayers, atomic reconstruction, the spontaneous structural relaxation toward energy-minimizing stacking registries in the near-commensurate regime, can strongly modify local stacking and interlayer coupling, providing a possibility to significantly control phonon-mediated properties. Here we show that the twist-angle dependence of interlayer thermal conductance can be modified by introducing Janus-induced mirror-symmetry breaking into bilayer MoS2. The intrinsic out-of-plane dipole in MoSSe/MoS2 bilayers leads to frictionless interface, and reduces the lattice deformation energy, thereby promoting atomic-reconstruction into locally distorted aperiodic moiré patterns. These features weaken interlayer coupling and suppress phonon transmission across the interface, leading to an anomalously strong twist-angle dependence of thermal conductance, with a pronounced minimum at small twist angles and a reduction rate nearly one order of magnitude larger than that of twisted bilayer MoS2. Our results demonstrate that interlayer thermal transport is modified by atomic reconstruction, highlighting Janus-induced mirror-symmetry breaking as an effective way to promoting phonon engineering in two-dimensional moiré structures.

</details>


### [150] [Electrical control of the metal-insulator transition in a one dimensional device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23138)
*J. Craquelin,L. Jarjat,B. Hue,A. Théry,C. Fruy,N. Struchkov,D. Stefani,M. M. Desjardins,A. Cottet,M. R. Delbecq,T. Kontos*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过在悬浮碳纳米管上施加空间调制的局域电势，利用与凝聚态系统的类比，实现了可调控的大能隙，该能隙在纳米管上均匀且可通过约两个数量级调节，使电子系统从绝缘态转变到近金属态，为量子信息处理中的退相干抑制提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纳米尺度下利用外部物理参数控制低能谱对量子器件至关重要，其中能隙的出现与抑制退相干直接相关。现有方法只能在特定调谐点缓解高能非受控激发的有害影响，缺乏在有限参数空间内实现退相干抑制的机制，因此需要一种能在较大参数空间中产生可调谐能隙的方法。

Method: 采用自上而下的方法，在悬浮碳纳米管上施加空间调制的局域电势，利用与凝聚态系统的类比来诱导能隙。通过调节调制电势的参数，实现能隙的大范围连续调谐。

Result: 实现了在碳纳米管上均匀分布的大能隙，该能隙可通过外部参数调节约两个数量级，使电子系统从绝缘态连续过渡到近金属态。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了通过空间调制局域电势在纳米管中实现可调谐大能隙的可行性，为构建超导拓扑链等量子器件提供了在有限参数空间内抑制退相干的有效手段。

Abstract: Controlling the low energy spectrum at the nanoscale with an external physical parameter has become an important resource for quantum devices. The emergence of an energy gap is one such key feature, linked to the mitigation of decoherence needed for quantum information processing. Indeed, the detrimental effects of high-energy uncontrolled excitations can only be cured at some specific tuning points in general. Achieving an energy gap is a natural way to extend decoherence countermeasures over a finite region of parameter space. This would be particularly useful in view of the recent efforts to build superconducting topological chains in a top-down approach. In this work, we demonstrate a large energy gap by spatially modulating the local potential of a suspended carbon nanotube, exploiting an analogy with condensed matter systems. This gap is homogeneous on the nanotube and tunable by about two orders of magnitude, bringing the electronic system from an insulating state to a near-metallic state at low temperatures.

</details>


### [151] [Signatures of Topological Magnon Edge States in THz Spectroscopy and Cavity Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23170)
*Ipsika Mohanty,Johannes Knolle,Silvia Viola Kusminskiy*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全光学方案，通过太赫兹泵浦-探测光谱学技术，利用参量放大机制实现铁磁拓扑磁振子绝缘体中手性边缘态的选择性探测，为拓扑磁性材料的实验研究开辟了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑磁振子绝缘体（TMIs）具有非平庸的体拓扑和手性边缘态，理论上支持无耗散自旋输运，但目前实验上难以直接探测这些边缘态，因为传统探测手段灵敏度不足。

Method: 利用磁电耦合机制，通过共振电磁驱动实现边缘磁振子的参量放大；考虑二维蜂窝晶格范德瓦尔斯铁磁材料中自旋依赖的有效电偶极矩，源于动态电荷涨落并符合晶格对称性（NNN相互作用），诱导出单光子-双磁振子过程；提出太赫兹泵浦-探测光谱学协议及太赫兹腔选择耦合方案。

Result: 边缘模式被选择性放大并通过吸收谱探测；太赫兹腔可进一步滤除体贡献，实现边缘模式的纯化探测。

Conclusion: 该全光学路线为探测拓扑磁体提供了可行方案，为磁性量子材料中奇异拓扑现象的实验室探索开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Topological magnon insulators (TMIs) have emerged as promising platforms for low-energy spin-based information processing, due to their non-trivial bulk magnon topology and robust, chiral edge modes that support dissipationless transport. Although theoretical models predict these edge states, direct experimental detection remains challenging due to their limited sensitivity to conventional probes. In this work, we propose an all-optical pathway to detect topological magnon edge modes in ferromagnetic TMIs. Our approach harnesses parametric amplification of edge magnons via resonant electromagnetic driving, enabled by magnetoelectric coupling mechanisms. We concentrate on two-dimensional van der Waals ferromagnetic materials on the honeycomb lattice with magnonic band gaps in the terahertz (THz) range. We show that a spin-dependent effective electric dipole moment, arising from dynamic charge fluctuations and consistent with the lattice symmetry up to next-nearest-neighbor interactions, gives rise to one-photon-two-magnon processes leading to parametric amplification. On this basis, we propose a THz pump-probe spectroscopy protocol in which edge modes are selectively amplified and subsequently detected in absorption. Furthermore, we discuss the possibility of using THz cavities, enabling selective coupling to edge modes while filtering out bulk contributions. These findings establish a route for probing topological magnets and open new avenues for experimental exploration of exotic topological phenomena in magnetic quantum materials.

</details>


### [152] [Current- and field-driven domain wall dynamics and chirality switching in a planar helimagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23252)
*Roman Teslia,Oleksiy Kolezhuk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究具有强易面各向异性的螺旋磁体中电流和磁场对手性动力学的影响，展示了畴壁可通过自旋转移矩的耗散（非绝热）分量被电流驱动，并证明了磁场中自旋转移矩的绝热部分会破坏手性的有效磁势能对称性，从而在特定条件下实现手性翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在具有强易面各向异性的螺旋磁体中，如何通过电流和磁场调控手性动力学，特别是手性翻转的机制。

Method: 基于准一维受挫铁磁体接近Lifshitz点的连续统理论，推导了自旋转移矩的非绝热和绝热分量对手性畴壁动力学的影响，并通过数值自旋晶格模拟验证结论。

Result: 1) 电流可通过自旋转移矩的非绝热（耗散）分量驱动连接相反手性区域的畴壁；2) 磁场中自旋转移矩的绝热部分破坏了手性相关的有效磁势能对称性，其主导对称性破缺项出现在磁化梯度的三阶；3) 推导出了手性翻转的条件。

Conclusion: 电流和磁场可协同调控螺旋磁体中的手性动力学，通过自旋转移矩的不同分量实现畴壁移动和手性翻转，数值模拟支持理论推导。

Abstract: We study the effect of electric current and magnetic field on the dynamics of chirality in a helimagnet with a strong easy-plane anisotropy. Using a continuum theory, derived for a quasi-one-dimensional frustrated ferromagnet close to the Lifshitz point, we show that a domain wall connecting domains with opposite chiralities can be driven by the current via the dissipative (non-adiabatic) component of the spin-transfer torque. Further, it is demonstrated that the adiabatic part of the torque in the presence of a magnetic field breaks the symmetry of the effective magnetic potential energy with respect to the chirality. We show that the leading symmetry-breaking term arises in the third order in the magnetization gradient, and derive the conditions of the chirality switching. Our conclusions are supported by numerical spin-lattice simulations.

</details>


### [153] [Current cross-correlations as probes for poor man's Majorana states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23498)
*Saatwik Patnaik,Aditya Saran,Himadri S Dhar,Pertti Hakonen,Thierry Martin,Bhaskaran Muralidharan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出利用电流互相关谱作为超越微分电导谱的新框架，用于在三个量子点构成的Kitaev链中验证真正的"穷人马约拉纳模"（PMM）的非局域性。研究发现，电流互相关在PMM甜点附近对外点失谐表现出显著稳定性，可作为短链极限下诊断真实PMM的可靠特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微分电导谱无法充分验证PMM模式的真正非局域性，需要发展新的诊断框架来区分真实的拓扑非局域态与其他局域态。

Method: 引入电流-电流互相关（cross-correlations）作为分析工具，研究PMM模式在偏离甜点时的退局域化行为，通过比较非局域输运过程的相对幅度来表征PMM的鲁棒性。

Result: 电流互相关特征在PMM甜点附近表现出对外点失谐的显著稳定性，能有效捕捉非局域输运过程的相对幅度变化，可作为短链极限下诊断真实PMM的可靠特征。

Conclusion: 电流互相关测量应作为验证纠缠态和拓扑保护态真正非局域性的标准诊断框架，是对现有微分电导谱方法的重要补充。

Abstract: The minimal Kitaev chain that emulates a topological superconductor with three quantum dots offers a tunable platform for potentially hosting poor man's Majorana (PMM) modes. Asserting the need to go beyond differential conductance spectroscopy, we introduce current-current correlations as a viable framework for verifying their true non-locality. The robustness of the PMM modes, specifically with respect to delocalization as the system is tuned away from sweet spots, we show, is embedded in the relative magnitudes of the nonlocal transport processes. This aspect is adeptly captured by current cross-correlations, whose features show remarkable stability around the PMM sweet spot, specifically with respect to the detuning of an outer dot. We establish this as a prominent feature and a diagnostic for true PMMs even in the short chain limit. Our results accentuate the need for current cross-correlation measurements as a diagnostic framework for unambiguously verifying true non-locality of entangled states as well as topologically protected states.

</details>


### [154] [Spin-Hall devices: spin relaxation spatially separates current injection from Joule dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23589)
*Yanbo Qiao,Sariah Al Saati,Jean-Eric Wegrowe*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过基于最小功耗原理的变分方法，研究连接外部负载电路的自旋霍尔条的稳态，发现当负载电阻距离大于自旋弛豫长度时，焦耳耗散消失，这是纯自旋电流注入最显著的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自旋霍尔条连接外部负载电路时的稳态特性，特别是纯自旋电流注入与常规电流注入的全局耗散行为差异。

Method: 采用推广到双自旋通道模型的最小功耗原理的变分方法，自洽推导纵向和横向电流密度分布、自旋和电荷积累以及电阻中的耗散功率。

Result: 当负载电阻置于距离远大于自旋弛豫长度处时，焦耳耗散消失为零。

Conclusion: 纯自旋电流注入展现出高度非平凡的全局稳态，其焦耳耗散消失特性与常规电流注入形成鲜明对比，是纯自旋电流的最显著特征。

Abstract: The stationary state of a spin Hall bar connected to an external load circuit is investigated through a variational approach based on the principle of minimum power dissipation generalized to the two-spin-channel model. The self-consistent distributions of longitudinal and transverse current densities, alongside the corresponding spin and charge accumulations and dissipation power in the resistance, are derived. Surprisingly, it is shown that the Joule dissipation vanishes when the load resistance is placed at a sufficiently large distance compared with the spin-relaxation length. Such a highly non-trivial global stationary state appears as the most striking characteristic of the injection of pure spin current compared with more usual current injection.

</details>


### [155] [Hidden topology and strong quantum metric bounds in trivial systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24208)
*Chang-An Li,Yulin Qin,Bo Fu,Jian Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种降维框架，将二维系统的量子度量积分（QMI）分解为低维分量，从而在传统二维拓扑平庸的情况下（如零陈数或贝里曲率处处为零），建立了由一维拓扑障碍导致的非零QMI下界。通过倾斜SSH模型和手性Wilson-Dirac模型验证了该机制，并进一步将Wannier带QMI与高阶拓扑不变量（如四极矩）联系起来，推广了量子几何与拓扑之间的基本关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上二维系统的量子度量积分（QMI）受陈数下界约束，但当陈数为零或贝里曲率处处为零时，该下界趋于平凡，无法提供有意义的几何约束。作者希望建立一种更普适的框架，在拓扑平庸情形下仍能为QMI提供非零下界。

Method: 提出一种嵌套循环式的降维框架，将二维QMI分解为低维分量；引入Wannier带基（通过嵌套Wilson loop构造），将高阶拓扑相中的QMI与高阶拓扑不变量关联。

Result: 在倾斜2D SSH模型和手性各向异性Wilson-Dirac模型中，QMI的下界由两个不同方向上的量子化Wannier带决定；在Benalcazar-Bernevig-Hughes模型中，Wannier带QMI下界由四极矩等高阶拓扑不变量决定。

Conclusion: 该降维框架在传统二维拓扑平庸的情形下建立了非零QMI下界，推广了量子几何与拓扑之间的基本关系。

Abstract: The quantum metric integral (QMI) in two-dimensional (2D) systems is conventionally bounded from below by the Chern number. For systems with zero Chern number or identically vanishing Berry curvature, however, this bound becomes trivial and provides no useful geometric constraints. Here, we develop a dimension-reduction framework that decomposes the 2D QMI into lower-dimensional components in a nested-loop way. With this method, we establish a nonzero lower bound on the QMI arising from one-dimensional topological obstructions even when the conventional 2D topology is trivial. We explicitly demonstrate this mechanism in a tilted 2D Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model and an anisotropic Wilson-Dirac model with chiral symmetry. The resulting lower bounds of QMI are determined by the quantized Wannier bands along two different directions. We further investigate the quantum geometry in higher-order topological phases following the same strategy. By introducing Wannier-band basis obtained from the nested Wilson loop, we demonstrate that the Wannier-band QMI is bounded from below by the higher-order topological invariant, e.g. the quadrupole moment in Benalcazar-Bernevig-Hughes model. Our results establish nonzero lower bounds on QMI from a dimension-reduction framework, thereby generalizing the fundamental relation between quantum geometry and topology.

</details>


### [156] [Chiral thermal fluctuations and enhanced refrigeration in a nonreciprocal nanomechanical system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24209)
*Jesse J. Slim,Javier del Pino,Sander A. Mann,Ewold Verhagen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过实验测量了非互易性对纳米机械谐振器网络中热流和制冷过程的影响，利用光诱导合成磁通控制非互易性，发现合成磁通可以调控热流分布并增强制冷效果，使谐振器温度低于时间反演对称网络的理论下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非互易性如何影响热流和微观热力学过程（如制冷和能量转换）是目前备受关注的问题。

Method: 通过时间调制的光机械相互作用实现受控耦合、规范场和制冷过程；使用光诱导的合成磁通控制非互易性；通过测量涨落相关性来量化非平衡热流和有效温度。

Result: 合成磁通在谐振器环路中赋予热涨落手性特征，在不同频带产生不同手性的手性流；非互易性增强了强耦合区谐振器的制冷效果，使其温度低于时间反演对称网络的约束下限。

Conclusion: 实验结果展示了非互易性对热力学机器的影响，并为在微观层面表征这些过程提供了新方法。

Abstract: Understanding how the breaking of reciprocity influences thermal flows and microscopic thermodynamic processes such as refrigeration and energy conversion is of broad current interest. We report experimental measurements of thermal flows and refrigeration in nanomechanical resonator networks in which nonreciprocity is controlled through an optically-induced synthetic magnetic flux. Time-modulated optomechanical interactions allow controlled coupling, gauge fields, and refrigeration processes through nanomechanical frequency conversion. We quantify nonequilibrium heat flows between resonators coupled to dissipative baths of different occupation and image heat and effective temperature in networks through measuring correlations of fluctuations. Synthetic magnetism is shown to imprint chirality on thermal fluctuations in a loop of resonators, leading to pronounced chiral flows with different handedness in distinct frequency bands. We find that the heat flows in a non-equilibrium system are tuned by the synthetic magnetic flux, which redistributes energy in the thermal steady-state. Specifically, we illustrate how nonreciprocity enhances the refrigeration of a resonator in the strong coupling regime, reducing its temperature below the bound that applies to time-reversal symmetric networks. These results experimentally demonstrate the impact of nonreciprocity on thermodynamic machines, and provide new methods to characterize them at the microscopic level.

</details>


### [157] [Large-area CVD Graphene for Photogating-Based Photodetection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24344)
*D. A. Matienko,D. P. Borisenko,A. Yu. Kuntsevich*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了CVD石墨烯作为可规模化光电探测器平台，通过基底诱导的光栅控效应在单层、双层和三层CVD石墨烯中实现光电导信号，最高灵敏度达995 A/W（单层），但层数增加会降低灵敏度；器件性能受光刻工艺影响，金属触点先于石墨烯转移制备的器件响应更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大面积CVD石墨烯作为可规模化光电探测器平台的可行性，并验证基底诱导光栅控效应在其中的主导作用。

Method: 制备单层、双层和三层CVD石墨烯/SiO₂/Si结构器件，通过光照（光子能量高于Si带隙）和栅压依赖性电阻测量，分析光电导机制，并比较不同光刻工艺（金属触点先于或后于石墨烯转移）对器件性能的影响。

Result: 单层CVD石墨烯最高灵敏度为995 A/W；随石墨烯层数增加，灵敏度因屏蔽效应和并联导电而下降，但噪声水平降低可提升探测率；金属触点先于石墨烯转移的器件表现出更高的光响应。

Conclusion: CVD石墨烯在光栅控型光电探测器中具有工业可扩展应用的潜力，器件性能可通过层数和制备工艺优化。

Abstract: Our work explores large-area CVD graphene as a scalable platform for photodetectors. We show that substrate-induced photogating in graphene/SiO$_2$/Si structures enables a photoconductivity signal in mono-, bi-, and trilayer CVD graphene. The devices respond to illumination with photon energies above the Si band gap and do not respond to telecom frequency, suggesting the photoconductivity mechanism through the photogating. Large sample area and gate voltage-dependent resistivity measurements allow us to prove the dominant role of the photogating directly, demonstrating similarity of the capacitive recharging current and the photoresponse. The sensitivity is the highest (995 A/W) for the monolayer graphene. Increasing the number of graphene layers reduces the sensitivity due to screening and parallel conduction, but can improve detectivity by lowering the noise level. The sensitivity of CVD graphene device depends on fabrication route of photolitography: device with metal contacts fabricated before graphene transfer show higher photoresponce. Thus, our work demonstrate the potential of industrially relevant CVD graphene for sensitive photogating-based photodetectors.

</details>


### [158] [Anomalous Localization in Magnetically Doped Two-Dimensional Topological Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24373)
*Felippe Amorim,Washington F. dos Santos,Mauro S. Ferreira,Alexandre Reily Rocha,Caio Lewenkopf*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立了描述磁性杂质稠密无序分布的二维拓扑绝缘体边缘输运的理论框架，发现了反常安德森局域化区域（电导呈次指数衰减 ∝ exp(-√L)）和普适标度行为，并在Mn掺杂HgTe量子阱中与实验数据高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立稀磁杂质对2DTI边缘输运的影响已得到充分理解，但稠密无序分布的磁性杂质体系下的输运性质尚不清楚。

Method: 发展了分析理论框架，结合大规模数值模拟，研究有限浓度磁杂质下的2DTI边缘输运；应用模型至Mn掺杂HgTe量子阱与实验对比。

Result: 预测了安德森局域化的出现，发现反常局域化区域（电导次指数衰减 lnG ∝ -√L），揭示了仅由有效杂质浓度决定的普适标度行为。

Conclusion: 为理解磁掺杂拓扑相中的反常局域化现象提供了理论基础，理论预测与实验数据吻合良好。

Abstract: Two-dimensional topological insulators (2DTIs) harbor spin-polarized edge states that are topologically protected by time-reversal symmetry against non-magnetic structural disorder. However, coupling to magnetic impurities breaks this symmetry, inducing backscattering and destroying perfect quantization. While the impact of isolated dilute magnetic impurities is well understood, the transport properties in the presence of dense, disordered ensembles of magnetic moments remain poorly understood. In this work, we develop an analytical framework, supported by extensive numerical simulations, that captures the behavior of edge transport in two-dimensional topological insulators (2DTIs) with a finite concentration of magnetic impurities. We predict the onset of Anderson localization and uncover an anomalous localization regime characterized by a sub-exponential decay of the conductance, scaling as $\ln {\cal G} \propto -\sqrt{L}$, where $L$ is the system length. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the transport exhibits a universal scaling behavior governed solely by the effective impurity concentration. Applying our model to Mn-doped HgTe quantum wells, we find excellent agreement with experimental data. These findings provide a theoretical foundation for understanding anomalous localization phenomena in magnetically doped topological phases.

</details>


### [159] [Electron Hydrodynamics and Bernoulli Effect in Venturi-Shaped 2D Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24386)
*C. A. Monari,A. D. Levin,A. S. Jaroshevich,Z. D. Kvon,V. A. Chitta,D. V. Dmitriev,A. K. Bakarov,G. M. Gusev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在超洁净二维电子系统中首次直接观测到强非线性输运 regime，利用Venturi楔形几何结构增强对流非线性，观察到明显的非线性电压响应和大二极特性，实验与基于电子流体对流加速（类比伯努利效应）的理论模型定量吻合，为电子流体动力学框架在二维系统中的适用性提供了有力证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子流体动力学为理解超洁净导体中的输运提供了强大框架，但此前实验证据主要局限于线性响应区，非线性流体动力学效应尚未被直接观测到。

Method: 设计具有Venturi楔形几何结构的器件以增强对流非线性，在高迁移率二维电子系统中测量电流-电压特性，并与基于伯努利效应的理论模型进行定量比较。

Result: 观察到显著的非线性电压响应和大二极特性，实验数据与电子流体对流加速（伯努利效应）理论模型定量吻合。

Conclusion: 这些结果为流体动力学框架在二维电子输运中的适用性提供了令人信服的证据，并开辟了探索固态系统中非线性和预湍流现象的新途径。

Abstract: The study of electron hydrodynamics provides a powerful framework for understanding transport in ultraclean conductors, yet experimental evidence has thus far been largely restricted to the linear-response regime. Here, we report the direct observation of a strongly nonlinear transport regime in a high-mobility two-dimensional electron system. By engineering devices with a Venturi-shaped wedge geometry specifically designed to enhance convective nonlinearities, we uncover a pronounced nonlinear voltage response and large diodicity in the current-voltage characteristics. Our experimental findings show quantitative agreement with a theoretical model that attributes the observed nonlinearity to the convective acceleration of the electron fluid, analogous to the Bernoulli effect. These results provide compelling evidence for the applicability of the hydrodynamic framework to two-dimensional electron transport and open new avenues for exploring nonlinear and preturbulent phenomena in solid-state systems.

</details>


### [160] [Testing edge chirality with a three-path fractional quantum Hall interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24451)
*Eugene V. Sukhorukov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种平均电流干涉仪，通过三个相干量子点接触形成的通量闭合环路，探测分数量子霍尔边缘态的方向性因果响应。该系统可区分下流和上流传播，并用于提取准粒子的电荷、标度维度和交换角等关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分数量子霍尔边缘态测量手段难以同时提取准粒子的电荷、标度维度和交换角等完备信息，且缺乏对边缘传播方向性的灵敏探测方案。本文旨在设计一种新型干涉仪，通过方向性因果响应同时测量这些关键参数。

Method: 利用三个相干量子点接触形成通量闭合环路，使主导阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆谐波为隧穿的三次方项。通过直接转移和相干两步路径之间的干涉分辨上、下流传播。分析局域Laughlin边缘（ν=1/m）的标度行为，引入弱有限程非局域密度相互作用激活上流系数，并推广到一般阿贝尔边缘态，推导方向性振幅的标度律和比值关系。

Result: 局域Laughlin边缘下游振幅标度为E^(3ν-2)，上游系数精确为零；引入非局域相互作用后上游振幅标度为E^(2ν-1)，相位为-π(1-ν)/2+χ_a (mod 2π)。一般阿贝尔边缘中方向性振幅标度为E^(3Δ_ℓ-2)，上下流振幅比|A^u_ℓ/A^d_ℓ|=|sin(πδ_-)/sin(πδ_+)|，交换角θ_ℓ=π(δ_+-δ_-) (mod 2π)。交叉电压排序可分离两个方向，折叠同填充Laughlin边缘实现符号可调桥接。

Conclusion: 该平均电流干涉仪可同时提取准粒子的电荷（通过阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆通量频率）、标度维度和交换角，为分数量子霍尔边缘态的全方位表征提供了一种全新实验方案。

Abstract: We propose an average-current interferometer for the directional causal response of fractional quantum Hall edges. Three coherent quantum point contacts (QPCs) form a flux-enclosing loop, so the leading Aharonov-Bohm harmonic is cubic in tunneling. Interference between a direct transfer and a coherent two-step path resolves downstream and upstream propagation. For a local Laughlin edge at $ν=1/m$, the upstream coefficient vanishes exactly, while the downstream amplitude scales as $E^{3ν-2}$. A weak finite-range nonlocal density interaction spanning the tunneling points activates the upstream coefficient without creating an upstream mode. At low temperature and unresolved delay, its amplitude scales as $E^{2ν-1}$ and its aligned phase relative to the downstream reference is $-π(1-ν)/2+χ_a$ modulo $2π$, with $χ_a=0$ or $π$. Opposite cyclic voltage orderings isolate the two directions, while a folded same-filling Laughlin edge with a neutral weak link realizes a sign-tunable bridge. For a general Abelian edge, the device probes the directional content of the selected tunneling vertex. If $δ_\pm$ are its downstream and upstream weights, its nonzero directional amplitudes scale as $E^{3Δ_\ell-2}$, with $Δ_\ell=δ_++δ_-$, and obey $A^u_\ell/A^d_\ell=|\sin(πδ_-)/\sin(πδ_+)|$. When both are nonzero, positive-flux alignment gives, after removal of a known fixed sign, the relative phase $πΔ_\ell=2πh_\ell$. In the same-vertex coherent unresolved-flight regime, the scaling dimension and directional ratio determine the exchange angle $θ_\ell=π(δ_+-δ_-)$ modulo $2π$. The Aharonov-Bohm flux frequency additionally gives the charge, enabling separate extraction of quasiparticle charge, scaling dimension, and exchange angle.

</details>


### [161] [Exceptional Cones from an Indefinite Bogoliubov Metric in Hyperbolic Polariton Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24456)
*Junhui Cao,Kirill Bazarov,Alexey Kavokin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了双曲极化激元凝聚体中玻戈留波夫度规的奇特几何结构，揭示了其如何将声学光锥转变为双曲稳定性楔形，并在非厄米驱动-耗散条件下产生异常锥面与双曲线，实现了可调控的非厄米例外简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索双曲极化激元能带中凝聚体所实现的、不同于普通玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体声学洛伦兹度规的集体几何结构，揭示其诱导的非厄米例外简并现象。

Method: 通过分析双曲极化激元能带中凝聚体的玻戈留波夫激发谱，推导其度规的时空特征；引入驱动-耗散与增益饱和效应，建立非厄米玻戈留波夫色散关系，研究零判别式曲面和异常锥面的拓扑结构。

Result: 双曲极化激元凝聚体的玻戈留波夫度规具有不定性，空间符号继承自能带曲率的相反符号；该度规将声学光锥转变为双曲稳定性楔形，区分传播模与动力学不稳定模。非厄米情况下，零判别式曲面成为参数空间中的异常锥面，在固定增益饱和时退化为异常双曲线，跨过该面能谱从传播转变为放大或过阻尼动力学。

Conclusion: 双曲极化激元凝聚体提供了一个可控平台，其中非厄米例外简并由有效的玻戈留波夫度规组织，为研究非厄米拓扑与度规几何的关联开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Long-wavelength Bogoliubov phonons in an ordinary condensate realize the standard acoustic Lorentz metric. We show that a condensate formed in a hyperbolic polariton band realizes a different collective geometry with an indefinite Bogoliubov metric whose spatial signature is inherited from the opposite signs of the band curvatures. This metric converts the acoustic light cone into a hyperbolic stability wedge, separating propagating quasiparticles from dynamically unstable ones. In a driven-dissipative condensate, gain saturation turns this metric relation into a non-Hermitian Bogoliubov dispersion. The zero-discriminant surface becomes an exceptional cone in the parametric space $(q_x,q_y,Δ_{\rm NH})$, appearing as an exceptional hyperbola at fixed gain saturation. Across this surface the Bogoliubov branches coalesce, the biorthogonal phase rigidity collapses, and the spectrum changes from propagation to amplified or overdamped dynamics. Our results identify hyperbolic polariton condensates as a controllable setting where non-Hermitian exceptional degeneracies are organized by an effective Bogoliubov metric.

</details>


### [162] [Skew scattering induced contribution to orbital Hall response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24671)
*Dayana Joy,Vivek Pandey,Rhonald Burgos Atencia,Pankaj Bhalla*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间反演对称性破缺的三维Weyl半金属中，无序诱导的轨道霍尔电导贡献。利用量子动力学方法分析了侧跳和斜散射的影响，发现高阶无序贡献（特别是无序势的三次方项）在振荡电场下主导了轨道霍尔电导，压制了其他散射机制。通过调节无序势强度、费米能量和Weyl节点间距可增强外在轨道霍尔电导，数值估算结果支持了轨道电子学器件应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索时间反演对称性破缺的Weyl半金属中无序对轨道霍尔效应的贡献机制，为轨道电子学器件开发提供理论依据。

Method: 采用量子动力学方法，系统分析侧跳（side-jump）和斜散射（skew scattering）贡献，考察无序势不同幂次对轨道霍尔电导的影响。

Result: 高阶无序贡献（无序势的三次方项）在振荡电场下主导轨道霍尔电导，压制侧跳等其它散射机制；轨道霍尔电导可随无序势强度、费米能量和Weyl节点间距调节。

Conclusion: 通过调控无序参数和材料参数可显著增强外在轨道霍尔电导，数值估算结果支持其在轨道电子学器件中的实验应用前景。

Abstract: Our study provides the disorder-induced contribution to the orbital Hall conductivity in three-dimensional Weyl semimetals with broken time-reversal symmetry. Using the quantum kinetic approach, we analyse the impact of side-jump and skew scattering contributions to the system. The dependence of the orbital Hall conductivity on both disorder potential and the Fermi energy is explicitly demonstrated. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the higher-order disorder contribution, especially from the third power of disorder potential, dominates the orbital Hall conductivity under an oscillating electric field in a time-reversal symmetry broken Weyl semimetal, suppressing other scattering mechanisms, including the side jump contributions. We can enhance the extrinsic orbital Hall conductivity by tuning the strength of the disorder potential, applied energy, and choosing the system with appropriate Weyl node separation. Finally, our results are supported by numerical estimations and highlight potential experimental relevance for advancing orbitronics device technologies.

</details>
